第 1 套 · 4 篇 · 共 2,401 词

Section A · 选词填空（填空已还原）293 词 · 14 句

词库（绿 = 本题所选）：A) aptB) burningC) extremesD) flatE) fractionsF) overflowingG) parasitesH) probablyI) sparinglyJ) sprinkleK) steerL) stimulateM) sturdyN) synonymousO) unique

The desert is deceiving.

沙漠具有欺骗性。

At first glance it looks lifeless, barren, and bone-dry.

乍看之下，它毫无生气，荒芜贫瘠，干燥至极。

For most passersby humming through the Mojave on their way to try their luck in Las Vegas or heading towards the Grand Canyon, it's just a flat stretch of land with some mountains in the distance and more apt to be a setting for a movie that takes place on Mars.

对于大多数驾车疾驰穿过莫哈韦沙漠、前往拉斯维加斯碰运气或驶向大峡谷的过客而言，这里不过是一片平坦的开阔地，远处点缀着几座山，倒更像是以火星为背景的电影取景地。

The desert, however, is overflowing with life, mystery, and magic.

然而，这片沙漠却充满了生命、神秘与魔力。

The Mojave desert sees less than two inches of rain a year, and like most deserts, is a land of extremes.

莫哈韦沙漠年降雨量不足两英寸，而且和大多数沙漠一样，是一片充满极端的土地。

Temperature fluctuations vary from freezing to burning hot, not only between seasons, but even within the same day.

气温从冰天雪地到酷热灼人剧烈起伏，不仅季节之间如此，甚至同一天之内也不例外。

At the heart of this vast, 25,000-square-mile desert is Mojave National Preserve.

在这片广袤的、面积达2.5万平方英里的沙漠腹地，坐落着莫哈韦国家保护区。

The folks managing and working there wear the wide-brimmed hats that have become synonymous with places like Yosemite.

在那里从事管理和工作的人们都戴着宽檐帽，这种帽子已成为约塞米蒂之类国家公园的标志。

The preserve is rich with history, culture, biodiversity, and endless opportunities to stimulate your interest, especially for anyone who loves the outdoors.

保护区历史底蕴深厚，文化丰富多彩，生物种类繁多，还有数不清的机会激发你的兴趣，对任何热爱户外的人来说尤其如此。

For a photographer, it requires patience and probably a few spare tires to maneuver the network of unpaved "roads", but the rewards are plenty.

对摄影师而言，要在这一片未经铺砌的“道路”网络中穿行，需要耐心，还得备上几条备用轮胎，但回报十分丰厚。

Photographing here requires a good, sturdy pair of shoes and a lot of preparation.

在这里摄影，需要一双结实耐穿的好鞋和充分的准备。

Plenty of water, spare tires, and a full tank of gas are needed to explore this unit of the national park system, which is the third largest in the country.

要探索这处国家公园体系中的园区——它是全美第三大的国家公园单元——必须带上充足的水、备用轮胎和满满一箱汽油。

Like all deserts, though, if you are patient, you will be rewarded, as they often reveal their secrets slowly.

不过，就像所有沙漠一样，只要你有耐心，终会有所收获，因为它们往往会慢慢显露自己的秘密。

During my last outing, I was surprised to see how much of the area was shaped by ancient volcanoes and geological forces, much of which remains today, giving the area a unique feel and painting a colorful background for great photographs.

在最近一次出行中，我惊讶地发现这片区域有如此多的地貌是由古代火山和地质力量塑造而成，其中大部分至今犹存，赋予了这片区域独特的韵味，也为精彩的照片描绘出色彩斑斓的背景。

Section B · 长篇阅读（信息匹配）1215 词 · 63 句

A

On the hills of central Kenya, almost lime-green with the sparkle of tea bushes in the sunlight, farmers know all about climate change.

在肯尼亚中部的丘陵地带，茶树丛在阳光下闪着光，满目青翠，这里的农户对气候变化了如指掌。

"The rainy season is no longer predictable," says one.

其中一位说："雨季已经没法预测了。

"When it is supposed to rain it doesn't, then it all comes at once."

该下雨的时候不下，要下就一次性全下下来。"

Climate change is an issue that will affect everyone on the planet.

气候变化是一个将影响地球上每一个人的问题。

For Africans its consequences will be particularly bitter: whereas other regions were able to grow rich by burning coal and oil, Africa will pay much of the human price without having enjoyed the benefits.

对非洲人来说，其后果将尤为惨痛：其他地区靠燃烧煤炭和石油得以致富，而非洲却要在未曾享受发展红利的情况下，承担大部分的人道代价。

"Africa only represents 2% of global greenhouse-gas emissions but it is the continent that is expected to suffer the most from climate impacts," says Mafalda Duarte, who runs the World Bank's $8bn Climate Investment Funds.

管理着世界银行80亿美元气候投资基金的玛法尔达·杜阿尔特说："非洲的温室气体排放量仅占全球的2%，但它却是预计受气候影响最严重的大陆。"

B

Although there are huge uncertainties as to the precise impacts of climate change, enough is known to say that global warming represents one of the main threats to Africa's prosperity.

尽管气候变化的确切影响存在极大的不确定性，但已知的情况足以说明，全球变暖是对非洲繁荣的主要威胁之一。

Parts of the continent are already warming much more quickly than the average: temperatures in southern Africa have increased by about twice the global rate over the past 50 years.

非洲大陆的部分地区升温速度已远超全球平均水平：过去50年间，南部非洲的气温升幅约为全球平均速度的两倍。

Even if the world were to cut emissions enough to keep global warming below 1.5℃, heat-waves would intensify in Africa and diseases would spread to areas not currently affected.

即使全球减排足以将升温幅度控制在1.5℃以内，非洲的热浪仍将加剧，疾病也将蔓延到目前尚未受影响的地区。

Farming would also be hit hard.

农业也将遭受重创。

About 40% of the land now used to grow maize would no longer be suitable for it.

目前用于种植玉米的土地中，约有40%将不再适宜种植玉米。

Overall, it is estimated that maize yields would fall by 18-22%.

总体而言，据估计玉米产量将下降18%至22%。

C

Africa is particularly vulnerable, in part because it is already struggling to feed itself and it will have to vastly increase yields and productivity if it is to put food on the plates of a fast-growing population, even without climate change.

非洲尤其脆弱，部分原因是它目前连养活自己都已十分吃力，而即便不考虑气候变化，要想让快速增长的人口都能吃上饭，也必须大幅提高产量和生产率。

The UN's Food and Agriculture Organisation reckons that by 2050 global food production would have to rise by about 70% over its level of 2009 to meet demand from a population that is growing in numbers and appetite.

联合国粮食及农业组织估计，到2050年，全球粮食产量必须在2009年的水平上增长约70%，才能满足人口数量和食物需求双双增长所带来的需求。

Much of this new demand will be in Africa.

其中大部分新增需求将出现在非洲。

Yet the continent already imports about $50bn-worth of food a year and that figure is expected to more than double over the next five years.

然而，非洲大陆目前每年进口的食物价值约500亿美元，预计这一数字在未来五年内将增长一倍以上。

Self-sufficiency is not Africa's goal, but the fact that it spends more money importing food than it does buying capital goods suggests it has room for improvement.

自给自足并非非洲的目标，但事实是，它用于进口食品的开支超过了购买资本货物的开支，这表明它仍有改进的余地。

D

Finding out why is not hard.

要弄清其中的原因并不难。

Most farms are tiny, ploughed by hand and reliant on rain.

大多数农场规模极小，靠人力耕作，且依赖降雨。

More than half of Africa's people make their living from farming.

非洲有一半以上的人口以务农为生。

Although its total harvest has climbed over the past few decades, this is mainly because there are more people farming more land.

尽管过去几十年间非洲的粮食总产量有所攀升，但这主要是因为从事农业的人口更多、耕种的土地更多。

But in many places there is no spare land to farm.

但在许多地方，已经没有可供开垦的闲置土地了。

Plots in Rwanda are so small that you could fit 250 of them onto the average American farm.

卢旺达的地块是如此之小，一个普通美国农场就能容纳250个这样的地块。

And although output per worker has improved by more than half over the past 30 years in Africa, that is still far behind the 2.5 times improvement in Asia.

尽管过去30年间非洲的劳均产出提高了五成以上，但仍远远落后于亚洲2.5倍的增幅。

Yields of maize are generally less than two tonnes per hectare, a fifth the level in America.

玉米的单产通常不到每公顷两吨，仅为美国水平的五分之一。

E

The low productivity of African farmers is reflected in national economic statistics—despite absorbing so much labour, farming generates just 15% of GDP.

非洲农民的低生产率反映在国家经济统计数据中——农业尽管吸纳了大量劳动力，却仅贡献了15%的国内生产总值。

"They can't even feed their families," says Jennifer Blanke, a vice-president of the African Development Bank in charge of agriculture.

"他们甚至养不活自己的家人，"非洲开发银行负责农业的副行长詹妮弗·布兰克说。

"Farm productivity hasn't improved in many parts of Africa for 100 years."

"在非洲许多地区，农业生产率已经100年没有提高了。"

F

One reason is that in the first few decades of independence, many African governments neglected farming as they focused on industrialising their economies.

原因之一是，在独立后的最初几十年里，许多非洲政府专注于经济工业化，忽视了农业。

Others damaged it by pushing down the prices that state monopolies paid for their crops in order to subsidise workers in cities with cheap food.

另一些国家则通过压低国家垄断企业收购农产品的价格，用廉价粮食补贴城市工人，从而损害了农业。

Ghana taxed cocoa exports so heavily that production collapsed by half between the 1960s and 1980s, despite a jump in the global price of cocoa.

加纳对可可出口征收重税，以致在20世纪60年代至80年代期间，尽管全球可可价格大幅上涨，产量仍下降了一半。

Yet over the past two decades or so governments and donors have begun to look again at farming as a way of providing jobs for the 13 million young people entering the workforce each year.

然而，在过去二十年左右的时间里，各国政府和捐助方已开始重新审视农业，将其视为为每年进入劳动力市场的1300万年轻人提供就业机会的途径。

Much of the focus has been on getting small farmers to use fertiliser and, more important, better seeds.

关注重点大多放在让小农户使用化肥以及更重要的——优良种子上。

The results can be impressive.

其成效可能相当显著。

Improved varieties of sorghum, for instance, can produce a crop that is 40% larger than the usual variety.

例如，改良的高粱品种可比常规品种增产40%。

Infrastructure is important.

基础设施也很重要。

A World Bank irrigation project in Ethiopia helped farmers increase their potato harvest from about 8 tonnes per hectare to 35 tonnes.

世界银行在埃塞俄比亚的一个灌溉项目帮助农民将土豆产量从每公顷约8吨提高到35吨。

G

Better techniques help, too.

更好的种植技术同样能发挥作用。

Small coffee farmers in Kenya are able to increase their incomes by 40% by following a few simple guidelines on caring for their bushes, such as trimming all but three of their stems.

肯尼亚的小型咖啡种植户只需遵循几条简单的咖啡树养护指南，例如每株只保留三根枝条、剪去其余枝条，就能将收入提高40%。

Many of their neighbours do not follow the advice, because it seems counter-intuitive.

许多邻居却不采纳这些建议，因为这样做似乎有悖常理。

More stems ought to lead to more coffee beans, they say.

他们说，枝条越多，结出的咖啡豆应该越多。

Yet after seeing those following the advice get bigger harvests for a season or two, many others start doing the same.

然而，在看到遵循建议的人一两个季度后获得更高产量之后，许多其他人也开始照做。

H

One way of spreading knowledge is to link farms to big buyers of their harvests.

传播知识的一种方式，是将农户与农产品的大买家对接起来。

When Diageo, a British drinks giant, built a brewery in western Kenya, it wanted to use local crops to make a beer cheap enough to compete with illicit home brew.

英国饮料巨头帝亚吉欧在肯尼亚西部建了一座啤酒厂，希望利用当地农作物酿造一种价格足够低廉、能与非法自酿啤酒竞争的啤酒。

It organised farmers into groups, improved supply chains for them to get seeds and fertiliser and then agreed to buy their grain.

公司将农户组织成合作社，帮助他们完善种子和化肥的供应链，然后承诺收购他们的谷物。

It now provides a market to about 17,000 farmers.

目前，公司为约1.7万名农户提供销售渠道。

Across the region it has doubled its use of local raw material to about 80% over five years, says John O'Keeffe, who runs its Africa business.

其非洲业务负责人约翰·奥基夫表示，五年来，公司在整个非洲地区使用的本地原料比例已翻了一番，达到约80%。

I

An even more important change is the move from traditional farming to building businesses that can profitably bring technology and investment to small farmers.

更重要的一项变革，是从传统农业转向构建能够盈利的商业模式，从而将技术和投资引入小农生产中。

Taita Ngetich, a young Kenyan, was studying engineering when he wanted to earn a little money on the side.

年轻的肯尼亚人泰塔·恩格蒂奇在攻读工程学期间，想赚点外快。

He scraped together 20,000 Kenyan shillings (about $200) to plant tomatoes.

他东拼西凑了两万肯尼亚先令（约合200美元），用来种植番茄。

Everything went wrong.

结果事事不顺。

The crop was attacked by pests.

庄稼遭到了虫害。

"Then there was a massive flood that swallowed all our capital," he says.

他说：“随后又发生了一场大洪水，把我们的全部本钱都吞没了。”

Mr Ngetich persevered by looking into buying a greenhouse to protect his plants from bugs and rain.

恩格蒂奇先生没有放弃，他开始研究购买温室大棚，以保护作物免受虫害和雨水侵袭。

The cheapest ones cost more than $2,500 each, so he designed his own for half the price.

最便宜的温室每个也要两千五百多美元，于是他自行设计，造价只需一半。

Soon neighbouring farmers started placing orders with him, and now his firm, Illuminum Greenhouses, has sold more than 1,400 greenhouses that provide livelihoods to about 6,000 people.

很快，邻近的农户开始向他下单，如今他的公司Illuminum Greenhouses已售出一千四百多座温室大棚，为约六千人提供了生计。

The business does not stop there; he also supplies fertiliser, high-quality seedlings and smart sensors that increase yields.

他的生意并不仅限于此；他还供应化肥、优质种苗以及能够提高产量的智能传感器。

J

Illuminum's success shows how technology can help even small farms become more productive.

Illuminum的成功表明，技术如何能够帮助即使是小型农场提高生产力。

Because such a large share of Africa's population earns a living from agriculture, even small improvements in productivity can lift the incomes of millions of people.

由于非洲有很大一部分人口以农业为生，生产力哪怕只有小幅提升，也能改善数百万人的收入。

But over the longer run small-scale farming can go only so far, especially in the face of climate change and population pressure.

但从长远来看，小规模农业的发展空间有限，尤其是在气候变化和人口压力面前。

K

"If we really want to lift people out of poverty we have to finance projects that will get them an income of at least $100 a month so that they can pay for health care and education," says Mr Ngetich.

恩格蒂奇先生说：“如果我们真心想帮助人们摆脱贫困，就必须资助那些能让他们每月至少获得100美元收入的项目，使他们能够支付医疗和教育费用。”

"Projects that give them an extra $2 a month from growing beans or maize aren't going to get them there."

“靠种豆子或玉米每月多赚2美元的项目，是无法让他们脱贫的。”

Getting those big jumps will need better jobs in factories and cities.

要实现这些大幅跨越，就需要在工厂和城市中提供更好的就业岗位。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 1449 词 · 27 句

P1

One of the great successes of the Republican Party in recent decades is the relentless propagation of a simple formula for economic growth: tax cuts.

共和党近几十年来最大的成功之一，就是不遗余力地传播一个简单的经济增长公式：减税。

P2

The formula doesn't work, but that has not affected its popularity.

这个公式并不奏效，但这丝毫没有影响它的受欢迎程度。

And while the cult of tax cuts has attracted many critics, it lacks for obvious rivals.

尽管对减税的崇拜引来众多批评者，但它缺少显而易见的替代方案。

P3

Democratic politicians have tended to campaign on helping people left behind by economic growth.

民主党政治家往往以帮助那些被经济增长抛在后面的人为竞选纲领。

When Democrats do talk about encouraging economic growth, they often sound like Republicans.

当民主党人确实谈到促进经济增长时，他们听起来往往像共和党人。

P4

This is not just a political problem for Democrats; it is an economic problem for the United States.

这不仅仅是民主党的政治问题，更是美国的经济问题。

The nation needs a better story about the drivers of economic growth.

美国需要一个更好的关于经济增长驱动力的故事。

The painful lessons of recent decades point to a promising candidate: higher wages.

近几十年来的惨痛教训指向了一个颇有前景的候选答案：提高工资。

P5

Raising the wages of American workers ought to be the priority of economic policymakers.

提高美国工人的工资应当成为经济政策制定者的首要任务。

We'd all be better off paying less attention to quarterly updates on the growth of the nation's gross domestic product (GDP) and focusing instead on the growth of workers' paychecks.

如果我们少关注国内生产总值（GDP）增长的季度数据，转而关注工人工资单的增长，我们都会过得更好。

P6

Set aside, for the moment, the familiar argument for higher wages: fairness.

暂且搁置关于提高工资的常见论点：公平。

The argument here is that higher wages can fuel the engine of economic growth.

这里要论证的是，提高工资可以为经济增长的引擎注入动力。

P7

Perhaps the most famous illustration of the benefits is the story of Henry Ford's decision in 1914 to pay $5 a day to workers on his Model T assembly lines.

要说这一好处最著名的例证，莫过于亨利·福特在1914年决定向其T型车装配线工人每天支付5美元的故事。

He did it to increase production—he was paying a premium to maintain a reliable workforce.

他这样做是为了提高产量——他支付的溢价是为了维持一支稳定可靠的劳动力队伍。

The unexpected benefit was that Ford's factory workers became Ford customers, too.

意想不到的好处是，福特工厂的工人也成了福特的顾客。

P8

The same logic still holds: Consumption drives the American economy, and workers who are paid more can spend more.

同样的逻辑至今依然成立：消费驱动美国经济，而获得更高报酬的工人也能花得更多。

P9

Mainstream economists insist that it is impossible to order up a sustainable increase in wages because compensation levels reflect the unerring judgment of market forces.

主流经济学家坚称，无法人为促成工资的持续上涨，因为薪酬水平反映的是市场力量的准确判断。

P10

The conventional wisdom held that productivity growth was the only route to higher wages.

传统观点认为，生产率增长是提高工资的唯一途径。

Through that lens, efforts to negotiate higher wages were counterproductive.

在这种视角下，通过谈判争取更高工资的努力反而是适得其反的。

Minimum-wage laws would raise unemployment because there was only so much money in the wage pool, and if some people got more, others would get none.

最低工资法会推高失业率，因为工资池中的资金总量有限，如果一些人拿得更多，另一些人就会分文不得。

P11

It was in the context of this worldview that it became popular to argue that tax cuts would drive prosperity.

正是在这一世界观的背景下，主张减税能够带来繁荣的观点变得流行起来。

Rich people would invest, productivity would increase, wages would rise.

富人会投资，生产率会提高，工资就会上涨。

P12

In the real world, things are more complicated.

在现实世界中，事情要复杂得多。

Wages are influenced by a tug of war between employers and workers, and employers have been winning.

工资受到雇主与工人之间拉锯博弈的影响，而雇主一直占据上风。

One clear piece of evidence is the widening gap between productivity growth and wage growth since roughly 1970.

一个明显的证据是，自大约1970年以来，生产率增长与工资增长之间的差距不断扩大。

Productivity has more than doubled; wages have lagged far behind.

生产率已经翻了一倍多，而工资却远远落在后面。

P13

A focus on wage growth would provide an antidote to the attractive simplicity of the belief in the magical power of tax cuts.

关注工资增长，可以对减税神奇魔力这一信念的诱人简单性起到解毒剂般的作用。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 2444 词 · 18 句

P1

Journal editors decide what gets published and what doesn't, affecting the careers of other academics and influencing the direction that a field takes.

期刊编辑决定哪些文章能够发表、哪些不能发表，这不仅影响其他学者的职业生涯，也左右着一个学科的发展方向。

You'd hope, then, that journals would do everything they can to establish a diverse editorial board, reflecting a variety of voices, experiences, and identities.

因此，人们会期待期刊竭尽所能组建一个多元化的编辑委员会，以反映不同的声音、经历和身份。

P2

Unfortunately a new study in Nature Neuroscience makes for disheartening reading.

遗憾的是，《自然·神经科学》上的一项新研究读来令人沮丧。

The team finds that the majority of editors in top psychology and neuroscience journals are male and based in the United States: a situation that may be amplifying existing gender inequalities in the field and influencing the kind of research that gets published.

研究团队发现，顶级心理学和神经科学期刊的编辑大多为男性，且驻地在美国：这种状况可能正在放大该领域已有的性别不平等，并影响何种研究得以发表。

P3

Men were found to account for 60% of the editors of psychology journals.

研究发现，心理学期刊的编辑中男性占60%。

There were significantly more male than female editors at each level of seniority, and men made up the majority of editors in over three quarters of the journals.

在各个资历层级中，男性编辑人数均显著多于女性，且在超过四分之三的期刊中，编辑的多数为男性。

Crucially, the proportion of female editors was significantly lower than the overall proportion of women psychology researchers.

关键的是，女性编辑的比例显著低于心理学女性研究者在研究人员中的整体比例。

P4

The differences were even starker in the neuroscience journals: 70% of editors were male, and men held the majority of editorial positions in 88% of journals.

神经科学期刊中的差距更为悬殊：70%的编辑为男性，且在88%的期刊中，男性占据了编辑职位的多数。

In this case, the proportion of female editors was not significantly lower than the proportion of female researchers working in neuroscience—a finding that reveals enduring gender disparities in the field more broadly.

在这一情况下，女性编辑的比例并未显著低于从事神经科学工作的女性研究者的比例——这一发现揭示了该领域更广泛范围内根深蒂固的性别差异。

P5

Based on their results, the team concludes that "the ideas, values and decision-making biases of men are overrepresented in the editorial positions of the most recognized academic journals in psychology and neuroscience."

基于研究结果，该团队得出结论："在心理学和神经科学领域最具影响力的学术期刊的编辑职位中，男性的思想、价值观和决策偏见被过度代表。"

P6

Gender inequality in science is often attributed to the fact that senior academics are more likely to be male, because historically science was male-dominated: it's argued that as time goes on and more women rise to senior roles, the field will become more equal.

科学界的性别不平等通常被归因于资深学者更可能为男性这一事实，因为历史上科学长期由男性主导：有人认为，随着时间推移，越来越多的女性晋升到资深岗位，该领域将变得更加平等。

Yet this study showed that even the junior roles in psychology journals tended to be held disproportionately by men, despite the fact that there are actually more female than male junior psychology faculty.

然而，这项研究表明，即便在心理学期刊中，初级职位也往往不成比例地由男性担任，尽管实际上初级心理学教职中女性人数多于男性。

P7

This implies that a lack of female academics is not the problem.

这意味着，女性学者数量不足并非问题所在。

Instead, there are structural reasons that women are disadvantaged in science.

相反，女性在科学界处于劣势有其结构性原因。

Women receive lower salaries and face greater childcare demands, for instance, which can result in fewer publications and grants—the kinds of things that journals look for when deciding who to appoint.

例如，女性薪资较低，还要承担更重的育儿负担，这会导致她们发表的论文和获得的资助更少——而这些恰恰是期刊在决定聘任谁时看重的东西。

Rather than simply blaming the inequality of editorial boards on tradition, we should be actively breaking down these existing barriers.

与其简单地把编委会的不平等归咎于传统，我们更应主动打破这些既有的障碍。

P8

A lack of diversity among journal editors also likely contributes to psychology's WEIRD problem.

期刊编辑缺乏多样性，很可能也助长了心理学的WEIRD问题（即研究对象过度偏向西方、受过教育、工业化、富裕、民主社会人群的问题）。

If journal editors are largely men from the United States, then they will probably place higher value on papers that are relevant to Western, male populations, whether consciously or not.

如果期刊编辑主要是来自美国的男性，那么无论有意无意，他们很可能更看重那些与西方男性群体相关的论文。

第 2 套 · 4 篇 · 共 2,410 词

Section A · 选词填空（填空已还原）296 词 · 15 句

词库（绿 = 本题所选）：A) activatedB) aggravatedC) ascertainD) deliberateE) differentiateF) discourseG) evokesH) inhibitI) manuscriptJ) marvelsK) remnantsL) scenariosM) speculativeN) unanimousO) uncertainty

Our brains respond to language expressing facts differently than they do to words conveying possibility, scientists at New York University have recently found.

纽约大学的科学家近期发现，我们的大脑在处理表达事实的语言时，其反应方式与处理表达可能性的词语时有所不同。

Their work offers new insights into the impact word choice has on how we differentiate between statements expressing what is real versus what is merely possible.

他们的研究为措辞如何影响我们区分「陈述的是真实情况」还是「仅仅是可能情况」提供了新的见解。

The researchers assert their findings are important because we are presented with false information all the time.

研究者指出，他们的发现意义重大，因为我们时时刻刻都在接触到虚假信息。

Some of this is deliberate, as is the case with deceptive advertisements, but the problem is aggravated by individuals who believe they are sharing correct information.

有些虚假信息是蓄意为之，比如欺骗性广告；而那些自认为在传播正确信息的人，更使问题雪上加霜。

Thus, it is more important than ever to separate the factual from the possible or merely speculative in how we communicate.

因此，在我们的交流中，区分事实性内容与可能性或纯属推测的内容，比以往任何时候都更为重要。

This is especially true as the study makes clear that information presented as fact evokes special responses in our brains, which are distinct from when we process the same content with clear indicators of uncertainty.

这一点尤为真切，因为该研究清楚表明，以事实形式呈现的信息会在我们的大脑中引发特殊反应，而这种反应有别于我们在处理带有明确不确定性标记的相同内容时的反应。

In their new study, the scientists intended to ascertain how the brain computes possibilities as expressed by words such as "may," "might," and "if."

在这项新研究中，科学家们旨在探明大脑是如何处理由「可能」「也许」「如果」等词语所表达的可能性的。

The researchers compared brain responses to statements expressing factual scenarios and those expressing possibility.

研究者比较了大脑对表达事实性情景的陈述和表达可能性的陈述的不同反应。

"There is a monster under my bed" exemplifies a factual statement.

「我的床底下有一个怪物」就是一个事实性陈述的例子。

"I will stay home," is also factual.

「我会待在家里」也是事实性陈述。

This is opposed to statements that express possibility, like "There might be a monster under my bed," or "If it rains, I will stay home."

这与表达可能性的陈述形成对照，例如「我的床底下可能有一个怪物」或「如果下雨，我就待在家里」。

The results of the study showed that factual language activated a rapid increase in brain activity, with the brain responding more powerfully and showing more engagement with factual phrases compared to those communicating possibility.

研究结果显示，事实性语言引发了大脑活动的快速增强；与表达可能性的语句相比，大脑对事实性语句的反应更为强烈，参与度也更高。

Thus, facts rule when it comes to the brain.

因此，就大脑而言，事实占据主导地位。

Brain regions involved in processing discourse rapidly distinguish facts from possibilities.

参与语篇加工的脑区能迅速区分事实与可能性。

Further, these regions respond in a much more robust fashion to factual statements.

此外，这些脑区对事实性陈述的反应要强烈得多。

Section B · 长篇阅读（信息匹配）1221 词 · 55 句

A

Most visitors come to Cape Canaveral, on the northeast coast of Florida, for the tourist attractions.

大多数游客来到佛罗里达州东北海岸的卡纳维拉尔角，都是为了这里的旅游景点。

It's home to the second-busiest cruise ship port in the world and is a gateway to the cosmos.

这里是全球第二繁忙的邮轮港口，也是通往宇宙的门户。

Nearly 1.5 million visitors flock here every year to watch rockets, spacecraft, and satellites blast off into the solar system from Kennedy Space Center Visitor Complex.

每年有近150万游客蜂拥至此，从肯尼迪航天中心游客中心观看火箭、航天器和卫星发射升空，飞向太阳系。

Nearly 64 kilometers of undeveloped beach and 648 square kilometers of protected refuge fan out from the cape's sandy shores.

近64公里长的未开发海滩和648平方公里的保护区，从这片海角的沙质海岸向外铺展开来。

B

Yet some of Cape Canaveral's most legendary attractions lie unseen, wedged under the sea's surface in mud and sand, for this part of the world has a reputation as a deadly ship trap.

然而，卡纳维拉尔角一些最具传奇色彩的景点却隐匿不见，被埋藏在海面之下的泥沙之中——因为这片海域有着“致命船舶陷阱”的恶名。

Over the centuries, dozens of majestic Old World sailing ships smashed and sank on this irregular stretch of windy Florida coast.

几个世纪以来，数十艘雄伟的旧大陆帆船在这段崎岖多风的佛罗里达海岸上撞毁沉没。

They were vessels built for war and commerce, crossing the globe carrying everything from coins to cannons, boxes of silver and gold, chests of jewels and porcelain, and pearls from the Caribbean.

这些船只为战争和贸易而建，航行于全球各地，运载着从硬币到大炮、成箱的金银、装满珠宝和瓷器的箱子，以及来自加勒比海的珍珠等各种货物。

C

Cape Canaveral contains one of the greatest concentrations of colonial shipwrecks in the world.

卡纳维拉尔角拥有世界上殖民时期沉船遗存最为密集的区域之一。

In recent years, advances in radar, diving, detection equipment, computers, and GPS have transformed the hunt.

近年来，雷达、潜水、探测设备、计算机和GPS的进步彻底改变了搜寻工作。

The naked eye might see a pile of rocks, but technology can reveal the precious artifacts that lie hidden on the ocean floor.

肉眼看到的可能只是一堆岩石，但技术能够揭示隐藏在海底的珍贵文物。

D

As technology renders the seabed more accessible, the hunt for treasure-filled ships has drawn a fresh tide of salvors and their investors—as well as marine archaeologists wanting to bring to light the lost relics.

随着技术让海床变得更易抵达，对满载宝藏船只的搜寻引来了一股新的打捞者及其投资者浪潮——还有希望让失落遗物重见天日的海洋考古学家。

But of late, when salvors have found vessels, their rights have been challenged in court.

但近来，当打捞者发现沉船时，他们的权利却在法庭上受到了挑战。

The big question: who should have control of these treasures?

核心问题在于：这些珍宝的掌控权究竟该归谁？

E

High-stakes fights over shipwrecks pit archaeologists against treasure hunters in a vicious cycle of accusations.

围绕沉船的高额利益争夺战让考古学家与寻宝者针锋相对，陷入相互指责的恶性循环。

Archaeologists regard themselves as protectors of history, and they see salvors as careless destroyers.

考古学家自视为历史的守护者，而把打捞者看作粗枝大叶的破坏者。

Salvors feel they do the hard work of searching for ships, only to have them stolen from under them when discovered.

打捞者则觉得，他们辛辛苦苦寻找沉船，可一旦发现，船就被别人从眼皮底下夺走。

This kind of clash inevitably takes place on a grand scale.

这类冲突不可避免地会演变成大规模的对抗。

Aside from the salvors, their investors, and the maritime archaeologists who serve as expert witnesses, the battles sweep in local and international governments and organizations like UNESCO that work to protect under-water heritage.

除了打捞者、他们的投资人以及出庭作证的水下考古专家之外，这场争夺还牵涉到各国政府、国际组织，以及联合国教科文组织等致力于保护水下遗产的机构。

The court cases that ensue stretch on for years.

随之而来的官司往往一打就是好几年。

Are finders keepers, or do the ships belong to the countries that made them and sent them sailing centuries ago?

究竟是谁发现谁拥有，还是这些船应当归属于几个世纪前建造并派遣它们出海的国家？

Where once salvors and archaeologists worked side by side, now they belong to opposing, and equally contemptuous, tribes.

打捞者与考古学家曾经并肩合作，如今却分属对立的两个阵营，彼此同样心存轻蔑。

F

Nearly three million vessels lie wrecked on the Earth's ocean floor—from old canoes to the Titanic—and likely less than one percent have been explored.

地球的海底散布着近三百万艘沉船——从古老的独木舟到泰坦尼克号——而被探查过的可能还不到百分之一。

Some—like an ancient Roman ship found off Antikythera, Greece, dated between 70 and 60 BC and carrying astonishingly sophisticated gears and dials for navigating by the sun—are critical to a new understanding of our past.

其中一些沉船——比如在希腊安提基特拉岛附近发现的一艘古罗马船只，年代介于公元前70年至60年之间，船上载有用于根据太阳导航的、精巧得令人惊叹的齿轮和刻度盘——对于我们重新认识过去至关重要。

No wonder there is an eternal stirring among everybody from salvors to scholars to find them.

难怪从打捞者到学者，人人都始终怀着发现这些沉船的热望。

G

In May 2016, a salvor named Bobby Pritchett, president of Global Marine Exploration (GME) in Tampa, Florida, announced that he had discovered scattered remains of a ship buried a kilometer off Cape Canaveral.

2016年5月，一位名叫鲍比·普里切特的打捞者——佛罗里达州坦帕市环球海洋勘探公司（GME）总裁——宣布，他在卡纳维拉尔角外海一公里处发现了埋藏在海底的一艘船的散落残骸。

Over the prior three years, he and his crew had obtained 14 state permits to survey a nearly 260-square-kilometer area off the cape; they worked 250 days a year, backed by investor funds of, he claims, US $4 million.

在此前的三年里，他和他的船员获得了14份州政府许可，对该岬角附近近260平方公里的海域进行勘测；他们一年工作250天，据他称，背后有400万美元的投资者资金支持。

It was hard work.

那是一份苦差事。

Crew members were up at dawn, dragging sensors from their expedition vessels back and forth, day in and day out, year after year, to detect metal of any kind.

船员们黎明即起，日复一日、年复一年地拖着探测仪在勘探船上往返作业，以探测任何种类的金属。

Using computer technology, Pritchett and his crew created intricate, color-coded maps marked with the GPS coordinates of thousands of finds, all invisible under a meter of sand.

借助计算机技术，普里切特和船员们绘制出精细的彩色编码地图，标注了数千处发现物的GPS坐标，而这些东西全都埋在一米深的沙子之下，肉眼无法看见。

H

One day in 2015, the magnetometer picked up metal that turned out to be an iron cannon; when the divers blew the sand away, they also discovered a more precious bronze cannon with markings indicating French royalty and, not far off, a famous marble column carved with the coat of arms of France, known from historical paintings.

2015年的一天，磁力仪探测到金属信号，结果是一门铁炮；潜水员吹开沙子后，还发现了一门更为珍贵的青铜炮，上面带有法国王室的标记；不远处，一根著名的大理石柱映入眼帘，上面刻有法国国徽，与历史绘画中的图案一致。

The discovery was cause for celebration.

这一发现值得庆贺。

The artifacts indicated the divers had likely found the wreck of La Trinité, a 16th-century French vessel that had been at the center of a bloody battle between France and Spain that changed the fate of the United States of America.

这些文物表明，潜水员很可能找到了"三一"号（La Trinité）的沉船残骸——那是一艘16世纪的法国船只，曾在一场改变美利坚合众国命运的法西血战中处于核心位置。

I

And then the legal storm began, with GME and Pritchett pitted against Florida and France.

随后法律风暴骤起，GME和普里切特与佛罗里达州和法国对簿公堂。

The Sunken Military Craft Act of 2004, a US federal act, protects any vessel that was on a military mission, allowing the originating country to claim their ship even centuries later.

2004年的《沉没军舰法》是美国的一项联邦法案，保护任何执行军事任务的船只，允许其所属国即使在数百年后也能主张对本国船只的所有权。

In 2018, two long years after Pritchett's discovery, the federal district court ruled in favor of France.

2018年，在普里切特发现沉船整整两年之后，联邦地区法院作出了有利于法国的裁决。

For Pritchett, the decision was devastating.

对普里切特来说，这一判决是毁灭性的。

Millions of dollars of investor funding and years of labor were lost.

数百万美元的投资者资金和多年的心血付诸东流。

J

But this is far from the first time a salvor has lost all rights to a discovery.

但这远非打捞者首次因一项发现而丧失全部权利。

In 2012, for instance, Spain won a five-year legal battle against Odyssey Marine Exploration, which had hauled 594,000 gold and silver coins from a Spanish wreck off the coast of Portugal across the Atlantic to the United States.

例如，2012年，西班牙在与奥德赛海洋探索公司的五年法律诉讼中胜诉。该公司曾从葡萄牙海岸附近一艘西班牙沉船中打捞出59.4万枚金银硬币，并将其运过大西洋带到了美国。

"Treasure hunters can be naive," says attorney David Concannon, who has had several maritime archaeologists as clients and represented two sides in the battles over the Titanic for 20 years.

"寻宝者往往过于天真，"律师戴维·康坎农说道。他曾代理过数位海洋考古学家，并在围绕"泰坦尼克"号的争夺战中代表双方长达20年。

"Many treasure hunters don't understand they are going to have to fight for their rights against a government that has an endless supply of money for legal battles that treasure hunters are likely to lose."

"许多寻宝者不明白，他们将不得不与政府争夺自己的权利，而政府拥有取之不尽的诉讼资金，寻宝者很可能败诉。"

K

Putting an inflated price on artifacts rather than viewing them as cultural and historical treasures that transcend any price is what irritates many archaeologists.

给文物标上虚高的价格，而不是将其视为超越任何价格的文化和历史瑰宝，这正是令许多考古学家恼火之处。

For the archaeologist, everything in a wreck matters—hair, fabric, a fragment of a newspaper, rat bones-all things speak volumes.

在考古学家看来，沉船中的一切都意义重大——毛发、织物、报纸残片、老鼠骨骼——所有这些东西都能说明很多问题。

Archaeologists don't want artifacts ending up in a private collection instead of taking humanity on a journey of understanding.

考古学家不希望文物最终落入私人收藏，而不是带领全人类踏上一段理解的旅程。

L

George Bass is one of the pioneers of under-water archaeology, and a researcher at Texas A&M University.

乔治·巴斯是水下考古学的先驱之一，也是得克萨斯农工大学的研究员。

He has testified in court against treasure hunters, but says archaeology is not without its own serious problems.

他曾在法庭上作证反对寻宝者，但也表示考古学本身并非没有严重问题。

He believes archaeologists need to do a better job themselves instead of routinely criticizing treasure hunters.

他认为，考古学家应该做好自己的本职工作，而不是一味地批评寻宝者。

"Archaeology has a terrible reputation for not publishing enough on its excavations and finds," he says.

"考古学在发掘和发现成果的发表方面做得远远不够，名声很差，"他说。

Gathering data, unearthing and meticulously preserving and examining finds, verifying identity and origin, piecing together the larger story, and writing and publishing a comprehensive paper or book can take decades.

收集资料、发掘并精心保护和检验出土文物、核实其身份与来历、拼凑还原事情的全貌，再到撰写并出版一篇全面的论文或一部专著，这一切可能耗时数十年。

A bit cynically, Bass describes colleagues who never published because they waited so long they became ill or died.

带着几分愤世嫉俗的口吻，巴斯谈到一些同行：他们一味苦等，等到病倒甚至离世，最终也未能发表任何成果。

Who is more at fault, Bass asks, the professional archaeologist who carefully excavates a site and never publishes on it or the treasure hunter who locates a submerged wreck, salvages part, conserves part, and publishes a book on the operation?

巴斯问道：到底谁更应受到责难？是那位一丝不苟地发掘一处遗址却始终不发表任何成果的专业考古学家，还是那个找到一艘沉没的船只、打捞出一部分、修复保存一部分，并就整个行动写书出版的寻宝者？

M

Pritchett concedes that his find deserves careful excavation and preservation.

普里切特承认，他的发现值得得到细致的发掘与妥善的保护。

"I think what I found should go in a museum," he says.

"我认为我找到的东西应该放进博物馆，"他说。

"But I also think I should get paid for what I found."

"但我也认为，我应该为自己找到的东西获得报酬。"

Indeed, it's a bit of a mystery why governments, archaeologists, and treasure hunters can't work together—and why salvors aren't at least given a substantial finder's fee before the original owner takes possession of the vessel and its artifacts.

的确，政府、考古学家和寻宝者为何不能通力合作，实在令人费解；而打捞者为何不能在原主收回船只及其文物之前，至少获得一笔丰厚的发现者酬金，同样是个谜。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 1449 词 · 16 句

P1

Could you get by without using the internet for four and a half years?

你能做到四年半不上网吗？

That's exactly what singer and actress Selena Gomez has done in a bid to improve her mental health.

这正是歌手兼演员赛琳娜·戈麦斯所做的，她以此来改善自己的心理健康。

P2

She has spoken extensively about the relationship between her social media usage and her mental wellbeing, recalling feeling like "an addict" when she became Instagram's most followed user in 2016.

她曾大量谈及自己使用社交媒体与心理健康之间的关系，回忆说2016年成为Instagram上粉丝最多的用户时，感觉自己像个“瘾君子”。

"Taking a break from social media was the best decision that I've ever made for my mental health", says she.

她说：“暂时远离社交媒体是我为自己的心理健康做过的最佳决定。”

"The unnecessary hate and comparisons went away once I put my phone down."

“一旦我放下手机，那些无谓的谩骂和攀比就都消失了。”

P3

Ditching the web at large, however, is a far more subtle and complicated prospect.

然而，彻底告别互联网则是一件微妙得多、也复杂得多的事情。

The increasing digitisation of our society means that everything from paying a gas bill to plotting a route to a friend's house and even making a phone call is at the mercy of your internet connection.

社会的日益数字化意味着，从缴燃气费、规划去朋友家的路线，甚至打电话，一切都取决于你的网络连接。

Actively opting out of using the internet becomes a matter of privilege.

主动选择不使用互联网，变成了一种特权。

P4

Ms Gomez's multi-millionaire status has allowed her to take the "social" out of social media, so she can continue to leverage her enormous fame while keeping the trolls at bay.

戈麦斯女士的千万富翁身份让她得以把社交媒体中的“社交”部分去掉，从而在抵挡网络喷子的同时，继续利用自己巨大的名气。

The fact that she's still the second most-followed woman on Instagram suggests it's entirely possible to maintain a significant web profile to promote various projects—by way of a dedicated team—without being exposed to the cruel comments, hate mail and rape or death threats.

她仍是Instagram上粉丝第二多的女性，这一事实表明，完全可以通过专人团队来维持一个颇具影响力的网络形象以宣传各类项目，同时不必面对那些恶毒评论、仇恨邮件以及强奸或死亡威胁。

P5

It goes without saying that this is fundamentally different from how the rest of us without beauty deals and films to publicise use the likes of Instagram, TikTok and Twitter, but even the concept of a digital detox requires having a device and connectivity to choose to disconnect from.

不言而喻，这与我们这些没有美妆代言和电影需要宣传的人使用Instagram、TikTok和Twitter的方式有着根本不同，但即便是“数字排毒”的概念，也需要先拥有设备和网络连接，才能选择断开。

P6

The UK's digital divide has worsened over the past two years, leaving poorer families without broadband connections in their homes.

过去两年里，英国的数字鸿沟进一步扩大，导致贫困家庭家中没有宽带连接。

Digital exclusion is a major threat to wider societal equality in the UK, so witnessing companies like Facebook championing the metaverse as the next great frontier when school children are struggling to complete their homework feels particularly irritating.

数字排斥是英国社会平等面临的一大威胁，因此，当学童们还在为完成作业而挣扎时，看到脸书这样的公司把元宇宙鼓吹为下一个伟大前沿领域，就让人格外恼火。

P7

Consequently, it's worth bearing in mind that while deleting all social media accounts will undoubtedly make some feel infinitely better, many other people benefit from the strong sense of community that sharing platforms can breed.

因此，值得记住的是，虽然注销所有社交媒体账号无疑会让一些人感觉好上无数倍，但也有许多人从分享平台所孕育的强烈社区归属感中受益。

P8

Internet access will continue to grow in importance as we edge further towards web 3.0, and greater resources and initiatives are needed to provide the underprivileged with the connectivity they desperately need to learn, work and live.

随着我们进一步迈向Web 3.0，互联网接入的重要性将持续上升；我们需要投入更多资源和举措，为弱势群体提供他们学习、工作和生活所迫切需要的网络连接。

It's crucial that people who feel that social media is having a detrimental effect on their mental health are allowed to switch off and for those living in digital exclusion to be able to switch on in the first place.

至关重要的是，那些感到社交媒体正在对自身心理健康造成不良影响的人应当能够断开连接，而那些被数字排斥在外的人首先应当能够接入网络。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 2444 词 · 17 句

P1

Psychologists have long been in disagreement as to whether competition is a learned or a genetic component of human behavior.

心理学界长期以来一直存在分歧：竞争究竟是后天习得的，还是人类行为中一种由基因决定的成分。

Whatever it is, you cannot but recognize the effect competition is exerting in academics and many other areas of contemporary life.

不管属于哪一种，你不得不承认竞争在学术领域以及当代生活的许多其他方面所产生的影响。

P2

Psychologically speaking, competition has been seen as an inevitable consequence of human drives.

从心理学角度来看，竞争一直被视为人类内驱力的必然产物。

According to Sigmund Freud, humans are born screaming for attention and full of organic drives for fulfillment in various areas.

按照西格蒙德·弗洛伊德的观点，人生来就会大喊着寻求关注，并且充满了在各个方面获得满足的本能驱力。

Initially, we compete for the attention of our parents.

起初，我们为争夺父母的关注而竞争。

Thereafter, we are at the mercy of a battle between our base impulses for self-fulfillment and social and cultural norms which prohibit pure indulgence.

此后，我们便受制于一场争斗——一方是追求自我满足的原始冲动，另一方是禁止肆意放纵的社会与文化规范。

P3

Current work in anthropology has suggested, however, that this view of the role of competition in human behavior may be incorrect.

然而，当前人类学领域的研究表明，这种关于竞争在人类行为中所起作用的看法可能是错误的。

Thomas Hobbes, one of the great philosophers of the seventeenth century, is perhaps best remembered for his characterization of the "natural world," that is, the world before the imposition of the will of humanity, as being "nasty, brutish, and short."

托马斯·霍布斯是十七世纪伟大的哲学家之一，他最广为人知的或许是他对“自然世界”（即人类意志尚未施加影响之前的世界）的描述——“肮脏、野蛮而短暂”。

This image of the pre-rational world is still widely held, reinforced by Charles Darwin's highly influential work, The Origin of Species, which established the doctrine of natural selection.

这种关于前理性世界的形象至今仍被广泛接受，并因查尔斯·达尔文极具影响力的著作《物种起源》而得到强化；该书确立了自然选择学说。

This doctrine, which takes for granted that those species best able to adapt to and master the natural environment in which they live will survive, has suggested that the struggle for survival is an inherent human trait which determines a person's success.

这一学说理所当然地认为，那些最能适应并驾驭其所处自然环境的物种方能存活；据此，生存竞争被视为人的一种固有特质，决定着一个人的成功。

Darwin's theory has even been summarized as "survival of the fittest"—a phrase Darwin himself never used—further highlighting competition's role in success.

达尔文的理论甚至被概括为“适者生存”——这个说法达尔文本人从未使用过——进一步凸显了竞争在成功中的作用。

As it has often been pointed out, however, there is nothing in the concept of natural selection that suggests that competition is the most successful strategy for "survival of the fittest."

然而，正如人们经常指出的那样，自然选择这一概念本身并无任何内容表明，竞争是实现“适者生存”最有效的策略。

Darwin asserted in The Origin of Species that the struggles he was describing should be viewed as metaphors and could easily include dependence and cooperation.

达尔文在《物种起源》中断言，他所描述的生存斗争应当被视为隐喻，其中也完全可以包含依赖与合作。

P4

Many studies have been conducted to test the importance placed on competition as opposed to other values, such as cooperation—by various cultures, and generally conclude that Americans uniquely praise competition as natural, inevitable, and desirable.

许多研究都曾检验过不同文化对竞争相较于合作等其他价值的重视程度，研究普遍认为，美国人格外推崇竞争，将其视为自然的、不可避免的、值得追求的。

In 1937, the world-renowned anthropologist Margaret Mead published Cooperation and Competition among Primitive Peoples, based on her studies of several societies that did not prize competition, and, in fact, seemed at times to place a negative value on it.

1937年，世界著名人类学家玛格丽特·米德出版了《原始民族中的合作与竞争》一书，该书基于她对若干不崇尚竞争、甚至有时对竞争持负面看法的社会的研究。

One such society was the Zuni Indians of Arizona, and they, Mead found, valued cooperation far more than competition.

其中一个社会就是亚利桑那州的祖尼印第安人，米德发现，他们对合作的重视远胜于竞争。

After studying dozens of such cultures, Mead's final conclusion was that competitiveness is a culturally created aspect of human behavior, and that its prevalence in a particular society is relative to how that society values it.

在研究了数十个这样的文化之后，米德得出的最终结论是：竞争意识是人类行为的一个文化塑造的面向，其在某一特定社会中的盛行程度取决于该社会对它的重视程度。

第 3 套 · 4 篇 · 共 2,396 词

Section A · 选词填空（填空已还原）274 词 · 15 句

词库（绿 = 本题所选）：A) correlationB) denseC) deterD) equityE) fosterF) imperativeG) imposeH) irrespectiveI) longevityJ) navigateK) recreationalL) rottenM) sprawlN) vibrateO) vicinity

Modern U.S. cities were designed to make exercise unnecessary.

现代美国城市在设计之初，就是为了让人们无须运动。

Cars and elevators once symbolized urban areas as machines for more efficient living.

汽车和电梯曾被视为城市作为高效生活机器的象征。

Now it is clear that these improvements provide great benefits but also impose health costs.

如今人们已经清楚地认识到，这些进步虽然带来了巨大的便利，却也付出了健康的代价。

Recent studies show that urban sprawl encourages more driving and is associated with heavier weight.

近期研究表明，城市无序蔓延会促使人们更多地驾车，并与体重偏重相关联。

This correlation suggests that the layout and design of cities can hinder or promote healthier lifestyle choices and it is imperative that urban planners bear this in mind.

这种相关性表明，城市的布局与设计会阻碍或促进更健康的生活方式选择，城市规划者务必将这一点铭记于心。

Unfortunately, urban planning still centers in large part on solving the problems of the past.

遗憾的是，城市规划在很大程度上仍以解决过去的问题为中心。

Of course cities still need to foster standard public health practices, such as separating toxic facilities from homes and restricting heavy truck traffic through dense residential areas.

诚然，城市仍需推动常规的公共卫生措施，例如将有毒设施与住宅区隔离，以及限制重型卡车穿越人口密集的住宅区。

But it's also important to create healthier cities—and the discussion is already underway.

但打造更健康的城市同样重要——而相关讨论已经展开。

More and more city planners are paying increasing attention to encouraging physical activity by making it easier and safer for people to recreate, walk, bike and take public transportation.

越来越多的城市规划者正日益重视通过让人们更便捷、更安全地进行休闲、步行、骑车和乘坐公共交通，来鼓励体育活动。

Many studies of longevity show that people live the longest in environments where physical activity is part of everyday life.

许多关于长寿的研究表明，在体育活动融入日常生活的环境中，人们最为长寿。

Providing more walkable spaces, better protected bike lanes and more recreational spaces are important steps.

提供更多适合步行的空间、防护更好的自行车道以及更多的休闲场所，是重要的举措。

But even smaller changes can be effective.

但即便是更小的改变，也能起到效果。

Cities can close off streets on weekends to encourage communities to get out and walk.

城市可以在周末封闭街道，鼓励社区居民走出家门、步行活动。

They also can provide more seating in public places, so that less-fit residents can rest during their journeys.

它们还可以在公共场所增设座椅，让体能较弱的居民在途中得以休息。

Using public spaces in cities as places where people can exercise promotes equity, rather than allowing physical activity to become restricted to private gyms with often-expensive monthly fees that deter less wealthy people from joining.

把城市公共空间用作人们锻炼身体的场所，有助于促进公平，而不是让体育活动仅限于私人健身房——这些健身房的月费往往昂贵，令经济条件较差的人望而却步。

Section B · 长篇阅读（信息匹配）1226 词 · 59 句

A

You may have heard that learning another language is one method for preventing or at least postponing the onset of dementia.

你可能听说过，学习另一门语言是预防——或至少延缓——痴呆症发病的一种方法。

Dementia refers to the loss of cognitive abilities, and one of its most common forms is Alzheimer's disease.

痴呆症是指认知能力的丧失，其最常见的形式之一是阿尔茨海默病。

At this time, the causes of the disease are not well understood, and consequently, there are no proven steps that people can take to prevent it.

目前，人们对这种疾病的病因尚不十分清楚，因此也没有经证实的、人们可以采取的预防措施。

Nonetheless, some researchers have suggested that learning a foreign language might help delay the onset of dementia.

尽管如此，一些研究人员提出，学习外语可能有助于延缓痴呆症的发病。

B

To explore this possibility more deeply, let's look at some of the common misconceptions about dementia and the aging brain.

为了更深入地探讨这种可能性，让我们来看看人们对痴呆症和衰老的大脑存在的一些常见误解。

First of all, dementia is not an inevitable part of the normal aging process.

首先，痴呆症并非正常衰老过程中不可避免的一部分。

Most older adults do not develop Alzheimer's disease or other forms of dementia.

大多数老年人都不会患上阿尔茨海默病或其他形式的痴呆症。

It is also important to remember that dementia is not the same thing as normal forgetfulness.

同样需要记住的是，痴呆症与正常的健忘并不是一回事。

At any age, we might experience difficulty finding the exact word we want or have trouble remembering the name of the person we just met.

在任何年龄，我们都可能一时想不起确切的措辞，或记不住刚刚认识的人的名字。

People with dementia have more serious problems, like feeling confused or getting lost in a familiar place.

痴呆症患者的问题则更为严重，比如感到意识混乱，或在熟悉的地方迷路。

Think of it this way: If you forget where you parked your car at the mall, that's normal; if you forget how to drive a car, that may be a signal that something more serious is going on.

可以这样理解：如果你在商场忘了车停在哪里，那是正常的；如果你忘了怎么开车，那就可能预示着出现了更严重的问题。

C

The idea that dementia can be prevented is based on the comparison of the brain to a muscle.

认为痴呆症可以预防的想法，是基于把大脑比作肌肉的认识。

When people talk about the brain, they sometimes say things like "It is important to exercise your brain" or "To stay mentally fit, you have to give your brain a workout."

人们谈论大脑时，有时会说“锻炼大脑很重要”，或者“要想保持心理健康，就得让大脑做做运动”之类的话。

Although these are colorful analogies, in reality the brain is not a muscle.

尽管这些比喻很生动，但实际上大脑并不是一块肌肉。

Unlike muscles, the brain is always active and works even during periods of rest and sleep.

与肌肉不同，大脑始终处于活跃状态，即使在休息和睡眠时也在工作。

In addition, although some muscle cells have a lifespan of only a few days, brain cells last a lifetime.

此外，虽然有些肌肉细胞的寿命只有短短几天，但脑细胞却能伴随人的一生。

Not only that, but it has been shown that new brain cells are being created throughout one's lifespan.

不仅如此，已有研究表明，人的一生中新脑细胞都在不断生成。

D

While it makes for a colorful analogy, comparing the brain to a muscle is inaccurate and misleading.

把大脑比作肌肉虽然生动形象，但这种说法既不准确，也容易引起误解。

So, if the brain is not a muscle, can it still be exercised?

那么，既然大脑不是肌肉，它还能被“锻炼”吗？

Once again, researchers don't know for sure.

同样，研究者目前也无法给出确切答案。

There are now many computer, online, and mobile device applications that claim to be able to "train your brain," and they typically tap into a variety of cognitive abilities.

如今有许多电脑、网络和移动设备应用程序声称能够“训练你的大脑”，它们通常会调动多种认知能力。

However, research suggests that although this type of training may improve one's abilities at the tasks themselves, they don't seem to improve other abilities.

然而，研究表明，这种训练或许能提升人在该项任务上的表现，却似乎无法改善其他方面的能力。

In other words, practicing a letter-detection task will, over time, improve your letter-detection skills, but it will not necessarily enhance your other perceptual abilities.

换言之，长期练习字母检测任务会提高你的字母检测技能，但这并不一定会增强你的其他感知能力。

E

However, there is some reason to believe that learning languages might be different.

不过，有理由相信，学习语言可能有所不同。

The best evidence that foreign language learning confers cognitive benefits comes from research with those who are already bilingual.

外语学习能带来认知益处，最有力的证据来自对已经掌握双语者的研究。

Bilingualism most commonly occurs when children are exposed to two languages, either in the home (mom speaks Dutch, dad speaks Spanish) or more formally in early schooling.

双语现象最常见于儿童同时接触两种语言的情况，无论是在家中（母亲说荷兰语，父亲说西班牙语），还是在早期教育中更正式地学习。

But bilingualism certainly occurs in adulthood as well.

但双语现象在成年人中当然也会出现。

F

Bilingualism and multilingualism are actually more common than you might think.

双语和多语现象实际上比你想象的更为普遍。

In fact, it has been estimated that there are fewer monolingual speakers in the world than bilinguals and multilinguals.

事实上，据估计，世界上只会一种语言的人比双语者和多语者还要少。

Although in many countries most inhabitants share just one language, other countries have several official languages.

尽管在许多国家大多数居民只使用一种语言，但另一些国家却有多种官方语言。

Switzerland, for example, has four official languages: German, French, Italian, and Romansh.

例如，瑞士就有四种官方语言：德语、法语、意大利语和罗曼什语。

Throughout large parts of Africa, Arabic, Swahili, French, and English are often known and used by individuals who speak a different, native language in their home than they do in the marketplace.

在非洲的广大地区，阿拉伯语、斯瓦希里语、法语和英语常常被一些人所掌握和使用，而这些人在家里说的母语与在市场上使用的语言并不相同。

So bilingualism and multilingualism are to be found throughout the world.

因此，双语和多语现象遍布世界各地。

And with regard to cognitive abilities, the research on those who speak more than one language paints an encouraging picture.

至于认知能力，针对使用多种语言者的研究呈现出令人鼓舞的局面。

G

For one thing, bilinguals are better at multitasking.

首先，双语者更擅长多任务处理。

One explanation of this superiority is that speakers of two languages are continually inhibiting one of their languages, and this process of inhibition confers general cognitive benefits to other activities.

对这种优势的一种解释是，双语者需要持续抑制其中一种语言，而这种抑制过程能为其他活动带来普遍的认知益处。

In fact, bilingual individuals outperform their monolingual counterparts on a variety of cognitive tasks, such as following complex instructions, and switching to new instructions.

事实上，在各种认知任务中，双语者的表现优于单语者，例如遵循复杂指令、切换到新的指令等。

For the sake of completeness, it should be noted that the advantages of being bilingual are not universal across all cognitive domains.

为全面起见，需要指出的是，双语优势并非普遍适用于所有认知领域。

Bilingual individuals have been shown to have smaller vocabularies and to take longer in retrieving words from memory when compared to monolinguals.

研究表明，与单语者相比，双语者的词汇量更小，从记忆中提取词汇所需的时间也更长。

In the long run, however, the cognitive and linguistic advantages of being bilingual far outweigh these two drawbacks.

然而从长远来看，双语带来的认知和语言优势远远超过这两点不足。

H

If the benefits of being bilingual spill over to other aspects of cognition, then we would expect to see a lower incidence of Alzheimer's disease in bilinguals than in monolinguals, or at least a later onset of Alzheimer's for bilinguals.

如果双语的好处能延伸到认知的其他方面，那么我们预期双语者患阿尔茨海默病的几率会低于单语者，或者至少双语者发病时间会更晚。

In fact, there is evidence to support this claim.

事实上，有证据支持这一说法。

The psychologist Ellen Bialystok and her colleagues obtained the histories of 184 individuals who had made use of a memory clinic in Toronto.

心理学家埃伦·比亚韦斯托克及其同事获取了多伦多一家记忆诊所184名就诊者的病史资料。

For those who showed signs of dementia, the monolinguals in the sample had an average age of 71.4 years at time of onset.

在表现出痴呆症状的人中，样本中的单语者平均发病年龄为71.4岁。

The bilinguals, in contrast, received their diagnosis at 75.5 years, on average.

相比之下，双语者被确诊时的平均年龄为75.5岁。

In a study of this sort, a difference of four years is highly significant, and could not be explained by other systematic differences between the two groups.

在这类研究中，四年的差异具有高度显著性，无法用两组之间的其他系统性差异来解释。

For example, the monolinguals reported, on average, a year and a half more schooling than their bilingual counterparts, so the effect was clearly not due to formal education.

例如，单语者报告的受教育年限平均比双语者多一年半，因此这一效应显然并非由正规教育所致。

I

A separate study, conducted in India, found strikingly similar results: bilingual patients developed symptoms of dementia 4.5 years later than monolinguals, even after other potential factors, such as gender and occupation, were controlled for.

在印度开展的另一项研究得出了惊人相似的结果：即使控制了性别、职业等其他潜在因素，双语患者出现痴呆症状的时间仍比单语者晚4.5年。

In addition, researchers have reported other positive effects of bilingualism for cognitive abilities in later life, even when the person acquired the language in adulthood.

此外，研究人员还报告了双语能力对晚年认知能力的其他积极影响，即使该语言是在成年后习得的也不例外。

Crucially, Bialystok suggested that the positive benefits of being bilingual were only found in those who used both languages all the time.

至关重要的是，比亚韦斯托克指出，双语的益处只体现在那些一直同时使用两种语言的人身上。

J

But encouraging as these kinds of studies are, they still have not established exactly how or why differences between bilinguals and monolinguals exist.

然而，尽管这类研究令人鼓舞，但它们仍未确切查明双语者与单语者之间的差异究竟是如何产生、为何产生的。

Because these studies looked back at the histories of people who were already bilingual, the results can only say that a difference between the two groups was found, but not why that difference occurred.

由于这些研究是回顾已经具备双语能力的人的经历，因此结果只能说明两组人之间存在差异，而无法说明这种差异因何而生。

Further research is needed to determine what caused the differences in age of onset between the two groups.

还需要进一步研究，以确定是什么导致了两组人在发病年龄上的差异。

K

Other studies of successful aging suggest that being connected to one's community and having plenty of social interaction is also important in delaying or even preventing the onset of dementia.

其他有关成功老龄化的研究表明，与社区保持联系、拥有充足的社交互动，对于延缓甚至预防痴呆症的发生同样重要。

Once again, however, the results are far less clear than the popular media might lead you to believe.

然而，这些结果远没有大众媒体让你相信的那样明确。

Older individuals who lead active social lives are, almost by definition, healthier than their counterparts who rarely leave their homes or interact with others.

社交生活活跃的老年人，几乎从定义上说，就比那些很少出门或很少与他人互动的同龄人更健康。

So we can't really say whether being socially active prevents the onset of dementia, or if people who don't have dementia are more likely to be socially active.

因此，我们无法真正断定是社交活跃能预防痴呆症的发生，还是没有患痴呆症的人更有可能保持社交活跃。

L

But even if studying a foreign language is not a magical cure-all, there is one thing it will do: It will make you a better speaker of a foreign language.

但即使学习外语并非什么包治百病的灵丹妙药，有一点是确定无疑的：它会让你的外语说得更好。

Doing that confers a whole host of advantages we do know about.

而这一点能带来一大堆我们确实了解的益处。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 1448 词 · 25 句

P1

Research is meant to benefit society by raising public awareness and creating products and innovations that enhance development.

研究的意义在于服务社会：提升公众认知，并开发出促进发展的产品和创新成果。

For research to serve its full purpose, the results must leave the confines of research laboratories and academic journals.

研究要充分发挥其作用，研究成果就不能局限于研究实验室和学术期刊之内。

P2

Findings effectively communicated can go a long way to serve the interests of the public.

有效传播的研究发现能够在很大程度上维护公众利益。

They can help address social injustices or improve treatments offered to patients.

它们有助于解决社会不公问题，或改善对患者的治疗。

P3

Many researchers seem to be content with sharing the results of their studies in academic journals or at conferences.

许多研究者似乎满足于将研究结果发表在学术期刊上或在学术会议上分享。

But few journals allow everybody to read the findings.

然而，能够允许所有人阅读研究发现的期刊寥寥无几。

Even articles freely available are usually written in academic language incomprehensible to the average reader.

即使是可免费获取的文章，通常也以学术语言撰写，普通读者难以理解。

P4

For researchers in the tenure-track system, their main goal is winning tenure, which in part can be achieved by getting a number of papers published in prominent journals.

对于身处终身教职轨道的研究者来说，他们的主要目标是获得终身教职，而在知名期刊上发表一定数量的论文是实现这一目标的途径之一。

Pressures like this mean community-level outreach is not prioritised.

这样的压力意味着面向社区的科普工作得不到优先重视。

P5

Many researchers lack the writing skills to describe their results to a general audience.

许多研究者缺乏向普通大众描述研究成果的写作能力。

They may also worry about whether the public will understand their findings, or about findings being used to influence controversial policies.

他们也可能担心公众能否理解自己的研究发现，或者担心研究成果被用来影响存在争议的政策。

These concerns cause some researchers to shy away from communicating their findings outside the academic community.

这些顾虑使一些研究者不愿在学术圈之外传播自己的研究发现。

P6

Propagating research findings beyond academic publications is particularly crucial for addressing certain social discrepancies.

将研究成果传播到学术出版物之外，对于消除某些社会差距尤为重要。

It can help families, communities, healthcare providers, policymakers, government agencies and other stakeholders to understand and respond to crises that plague society.

这有助于家庭、社区、医疗服务提供者、政策制定者、政府机构及其他利益相关方理解并应对困扰社会的各种危机。

P7

The benefits of sharing findings flow both ways.

分享研究成果的好处是双向的。

Engaging with other researchers and the public can lead to unexpected new connections and new ideas that could suggest fruitful new directions for research.

与其他研究者和公众交流互动，可能带来意想不到的新联系和新想法，为研究指出富有成效的新方向。

P8

To benefit both researchers and the communities, the need to find innovative, accessible ways to share the work cannot be overstated.

为了让研究者和社区双方都受益，必须想方设法以创新、易懂的方式分享研究工作，这一点再怎么强调都不为过。

P9

Institutions and funding organisations should support more researchers to publish in open access journals so that the public doesn't have to pay to read them.

各研究机构和资助组织应支持更多研究者在开放获取期刊上发表论文，使公众无需付费即可阅读。

Institutions and researchers should invest in partnerships that expand capacity for sharing results more broadly.

各机构和研究者应投资于合作关系，以扩大更广泛地分享研究成果的能力。

P10

Furthermore, ethics committees should make it mandatory for researchers to share their results with the public.

此外，伦理委员会应强制要求研究者向公众公开其研究结果。

Every research participant should opt in or out of receiving results, as part of the process of giving informed consent.

每一位研究参与者都应在知情同意过程中，自主选择是否接收研究结果。

P11

There could be misunderstanding of the findings presented by the researcher because of technical terms.

由于专业术语的存在，研究者所呈现的研究发现可能会引发误解。

But this can be resolved by researchers engaging the services of professional writers or communication officers to help with translating their study into more accessible language and share it widely with media outlets and the public.

但研究者可以聘请专业撰稿人或传播专员，协助将研究内容转化为更通俗易懂的语言，并通过媒体和公众广泛传播，从而解决这一问题。

P12

Sharing results with the people who are most affected by them makes us better researchers and ensures that our work can be used to improve people's lives.

与受研究结果影响最深的人群分享成果，能使我们成为更好的研究者，并确保我们的工作能够切实改善人们的生活。

Institutions and collaborators must recognise the value of doing so.

各机构及其合作者必须认识到这样做的价值。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 2448 词 · 21 句

P1

Spiders make their presence felt in late August and through early autumn.

蜘蛛在八月下旬到初秋这段时间尤为引人注目。

This is the mating season of some of the most common varieties, when male house spiders come out of hidden corners to look for females, and garden spiders reach adult size and spin their most dazzling webs.

这是某些最常见蜘蛛品种的交配季节，雄性家蛛会从隐蔽的角落爬出来寻找雌蛛，园蛛也长到成体大小，编织出最为华丽的蛛网。

P2

Yet while the spider is a familiar fixture of nursery poems or songs and Halloween decorations, its relationship with humans is complicated.

然而，尽管蜘蛛是童谣童歌和万圣节装饰中再熟悉不过的形象，它与人类的关系却并不简单。

Fear of spiders is common and has serious impacts on the lives of sufferers.

对蜘蛛的恐惧十分普遍，且严重影响着患者的生活。

Its prevalence appears unrelated to any rational assessment of risk.

这种恐惧的广泛存在似乎与任何理性的风险评估无关。

Spiders in the UK are almost all harmless.

英国的蜘蛛几乎全都无害。

Farmland species perform valuable ecosystem services, by preying on insects that are our competitors for crops.

农田蜘蛛种类通过捕食与人类争夺作物的昆虫，提供了宝贵的生态服务。

But they are a constant source of human anxieties—with a cultural association with witches and wickedness dating back to the middle ages.

但它们始终是人类焦虑的来源——自中世纪以来，蜘蛛就与女巫和邪恶有着文化上的关联。

P3

Does this perhaps explain, in part, the lack of data about how spiders are faring in our age of ecological crisis?

这是否在一定程度上解释了为何我们缺乏蜘蛛在当今生态危机时代生存状况的数据？

British butterflies are the most studied group of insects in the world, due to the long tradition of collecting and observing them.

由于长期的采集和观察传统，英国蝴蝶是全球被研究最多的昆虫类群。

But spider conservationists point out that it was only in the 1980s that the classification of house spiders was properly sorted out.

但蜘蛛保护人士指出，直到20世纪80年代，家蛛的分类才得到妥善梳理。

And while information about insect populations is gathered by experiments that measure the numbers hitting windscreens or traps, there have been few attempts to count spiders.

而且，虽然关于昆虫种群的信息是通过实验测量撞上挡风玻璃或落入陷阱的数量来收集的，但迄今鲜有人尝试统计蜘蛛的数量。

P4

The huge reductions in the numbers of flying insects can only mean a reduction in spiders' food supply.

飞行昆虫数量的大幅减少，只能意味着蜘蛛食物来源的减少。

A recent landmark study identified a 75% fall in insect populations between 1989 and 2016, with pesticide use thought to be to blame along with the destruction of wild areas for development.

最近一项具有里程碑意义的研究发现，1989年至2016年间昆虫种群数量下降了75%，农药的使用以及为开发而破坏野生区域被认为是罪魁祸首。

This means the overall picture for spiders is worrying, as it is for most creatures.

这意味着蜘蛛的总体状况令人担忧，大多数生物亦然。

But conservationists are most concerned about those varieties that are threatened due to habitat loss and fragmentation, which makes it impossible for them to migrate.

但自然保护人士最为担心的，是那些因栖息地丧失和破碎化而受到威胁的种类——栖息地破碎化使它们无法迁移。

P5

Of around 650 spider species regularly recorded in the UK, the majority thrive in marshes and wasteland.

在英国经常被记录在案的约650种蜘蛛中，大多数在沼泽和荒地上繁衍生息。

Conservation efforts, often led by determined individuals, have helped some species to recover by reintroducing them to new areas.

由意志坚定的个人牵头开展的保护工作，通过将一些物种重新引入新的区域，帮助它们恢复了种群。

With rewilding now firmly on the environmental policy agenda, the hope is that in future, spiders will be enabled to migrate by themselves, adapting to climate change by moving along wildlife corridors.

随着“再野化”如今已稳居环境政策议程，人们希望未来蜘蛛能够自行迁移，沿着野生动物廊道迁徙以适应气候变化。

P6

It seems unlikely that spiders will ever attract the same level of human enthusiasm as bees, birds or butterflies, in spite of their unique status as nature's spinners.

尽管蜘蛛拥有“自然界织网者”的独特地位，但它们似乎不太可能像蜜蜂、鸟类或蝴蝶那样激发人们同等的热情。

But as they reveal themselves in all their splendour this autumn, it would be a good thing if more animal lovers recognised the ways in which spiders are simply terrific.

但当它们今秋尽显其绚丽风采之时，若能有更多的动物爱好者认识到蜘蛛的种种了不起之处，将是一件好事。
