如果对话的气氛升温、愤怒爆发，对话就会从协作转向对抗，进展很可能立即中断。

In addition, effective dialogue generally has another important characteristic: symmetry.

此外，有效的对话通常还有一个重要特征：对等性。

Symmetry in dialogue refers to the balance of how much each partner talks.

对话中的对等性是指双方说话量的平衡。

Are you each getting equal airtime?

你们各自拥有同等的发言机会吗？

When one participant does most of the talking, this asymmetry or lack of balance tends to become annoying to one or both of you.

当一方占据大部分发言时间时，这种不对等或失衡往往会让一方或双方感到厌烦。

The silent partner typically tires of only listening, while the talker can feel overburdened.

一直只做倾听的一方通常会感到厌倦，而滔滔不绝的一方则可能觉得不堪重负。

Usually, both would prefer more equal give and take.

通常，双方都希望彼此的付出与回报更加对等。

Different rates of speech and levels of voice volume can erode symmetry as well.

语速的快慢和音量的高低差异也会破坏这种平衡。

The faster or louder partner can easily begin taking up more airtime.

语速更快或声音更大的一方很容易占据更多的发言时间。

The slower-talking or softer-voiced one can have trouble getting the floor.

语速较慢或声音较轻的一方则可能难以获得发言机会。

Conversations also lose balance when one partner's opinions count more than the other's.

当一方的意见比另一方的更有分量时，对话也会失去平衡。

One person's input may carry more sway because their style of expression is more vigorous.

一个人的发言可能更具影响力，因为其表达方式更为有力。

One viewpoint may tend to get lost because it is expressed more tentatively.

某种观点可能会被忽视，因为它表达得较为委婉试探。

If either partner fairly consistently predominates or evaporates, modifying the pattern will be helpful.

如果其中一方长期占据主导地位或总是沉默退让，那么改变这种模式将大有裨益。

The dialogue will feel more productive, and at the same time, the relationship overall is likely to feel more positive.

对话会因此更有成效，同时，整体关系也往往会变得更加融洽。

Section B · 长篇阅读（信息匹配）1218 词 · 52 句

A

Blue moons, rose moons, supermoons.

蓝月亮、粉红月亮、超级月亮。

For some reason all the websites seem to think you really want to know all about these moons.

不知为何，所有网站似乎都认定你真的很想了解关于这些月亮的一切。

"Catch This Weekend's AMAZING SUPERMOON," one headline will announce.

“别错过本周末惊人的超级月亮”——某个标题会这样大声宣告。

"The Supermoon Isn't Actually A Big Deal And You're All Ruining Astronomy," another will complain.

“超级月亮其实没什么大不了的，你们都把天文学给毁了”——另一个标题会这样抱怨道。

B

The latest example is the super blood wolf moon eclipse which sounds like the name of an emotional song I would have performed a modern dance routine to in 9th grade, but is, in fact, an astronomical event set to occur on January 20.

最新的例子是“超级血狼月食”，这名字听起来像一首情绪化的歌名，九年级时我会伴着它跳上一段现代舞，但它其实是一场定于1月20日发生的天文现象。

As we'll explain below, there is nothing truly mysterious or otherwise special about this moon, and it will not look like a bloody wolf head in the sky or anything wild like that.

正如下文将要解释的，这次的月亮并没有什么真正神秘或特别之处，它不会在天空中看起来像一颗血淋淋的狼头，也不会有任何那般离谱的样子。

We get these super lunar events by smashing together all the qualifiers we've historically used to keep track of full moons throughout the year, and in the age of the internet we can get a little too enthusiastic.

这些超级天象，是我们把历史上用来区分全年各次满月的所有修饰语一股脑儿拼在一起造出来的；而在互联网时代，我们有时热情得过了头。

C

Consider this your go-to resource for all moon-gazing news.

请把本文当作你了解一切赏月新闻的首选指南。

Here's what you need to know about the latest lunar event.

关于这次最新的天象，以下是你需要知道的内容。

D

Look, it's okay if you don't know.

听着，你不知道也没关系。

There are probably loads of folks who walk around pretending they totally know why that thing in the sky seems to get bigger and smaller at regular intervals but who totally do not.

恐怕有很多人整天装作完全明白天上那个东西为何会定期变大变小，其实他们压根儿就不明白。

E

The moon orbits Earth, and it's tidally locked—that means it always shows us the same face, instead of spinning around like our planet does.

月球绕地球运行，并且被潮汐锁定——这意味着它始终以同一面朝向我们，而不会像我们的星球那样旋转不停。

That's why you can always see the man on the moon (or the moon rabbit, depending on your cultural preferences) even as it spins around us.

这就是为什么，无论月亮如何在我们周围旋转，你总能看到月亮上的那个男人（或者是玉兔，取决于你的文化偏好）。

But while the moon is big and bright in the sky when it's full, that's only because it's reflecting light from the sun.

不过，满月时月亮虽然又大又亮，那只是因为它在反射太阳的光。

But the moon is always moving, so it's getting hit with sunlight at different angles.

但月亮始终在移动，因此阳光照射它的角度也在不断变化。

It's invisible to us during the new moon, because our satellite is parked right between us and the sun; the so-called dark side of the moon is lit up like Las Vegas, but the side we can see is in shadow.

新月时我们看不到它，因为此时月球正好位于我们和太阳之间；所谓的月球背面被照得像拉斯维加斯一样亮，而我们能看到的一面却处在阴影中。

A full moon happens when the earth is right between the sun and the moon, so sunlight hits the part we can see.

满月发生在地球恰好位于太阳和月亮之间时，此时阳光照在我们能看到的那一面。

And all the other phases are just the transition from one of those extremes to the other.

而月相的所有其他变化，不过是这两种极端状态之间的过渡。

F

The moon isn't always exactly the same distance from Earth, because its orbit isn't perfectly circular.

月亮与地球的距离并非始终完全相同，因为它的轨道并不是完美的圆形。

We call the closest point perigee, and the most distant point is apogee.

我们把最近的点称为近地点，最远的点称为远地点。

2018's closest perigee and most distant apogee both happened in January, and the difference was about 30,000 miles.

2018年最近的近地点和最远的远地点都出现在一月，两者相差约三万英里。

G

The reason you care about this ordinary change in distance is that it turns a moon super.

你之所以要在意这个再寻常不过的距离变化，是因为它能让月亮变“超级”。

When a full moon happens close to perigee, it's going to look a bit bigger.

当满月出现在接近近地点时，月亮看上去会稍大一些。

Honestly, the difference is not that profound, but if you're in a position to photograph the supermoon next to something that shows the slight increase in scale, it can look pretty cool.

说实话，差别并没有那么明显，但如果你能拍到超级月亮，并在旁边放一个可以衬托出其尺寸略微增大的参照物，照片看起来会相当酷。

January's super blood wolf moon eclipse is super because the date lines up with the closest the moon will get to us during January, but the moon won't actually be at its closest for the year until February's supermoon, which you can expect plenty of undue excitement over.

一月的超级血狼月之所以“超级”，是因为这一天正好是月亮在一月里离我们最近的时候；但月亮全年真正离我们最近的一次，其实要等到二月的超级月亮，届时想必又会掀起一阵不必要的热潮。

H

Blood moons only occur during total lunar eclipses (which can happen a few times a year in any given location).

血月只在月全食期间出现（月全食在任何地方一年都可能发生几次）。

When the moon slips through our shadow, we give it a reddish coloring.

当月亮穿过我们的影子时，我们便给它染上了一层微红的色彩。

The moon can also look orange whenever it's rising or setting, or if it hangs low in the horizon all night—the light bouncing off of it has to travel through a thicker atmosphere there, which scatters more blue light away.

月亮在升起或落下时，或整夜低垂在地平线附近时，看起来也可能是橙色的——从月亮反射回来的光在这些位置需要穿过更厚的大气层，大气层将更多蓝光散射掉了。

But you'll probably only see that deep, sinister red during an eclipse.

但那种深沉而阴森的红色，恐怕只有在月食时才能见到。

I

A lot of headlines about moons are just ridiculous (you do not need to be particularly excited about a blue moon, it just looks like a regular full moon), but you should definitely roll out of bed to look at a blood moon if one is going to be visible in your region, even though they're just lunar eclipses and not evidence of bloody battles between the sky gods.

关于月亮的许多新闻标题简直荒唐可笑（你实在不必为蓝月格外激动，它看上去就跟平常的满月一样），但如果血月将在你所在地区可见，你绝对应该爬下床去看一看——尽管它们不过是月全食，并不是天神之间血腥大战的证据。

J

In March of 2018, we had our second "blue moon" of the year, to much acclaim.

2018年3月，我们迎来了当年的第二次“蓝月”，备受瞩目。

And while that's not necessarily special in an oh-gosh-get-out-and-look-at-it way, it's certainly special: a blue moon is a nickname for when two full moons fall in the same calendar month, and we hadn't previously had two in one year since 1999.

虽然这未必值得“天啊快出去看看”那种兴奋，但它确实非同寻常：所谓蓝月，是指同一个日历月内出现两次满月，而上一次一年之内出现两次蓝月，还要追溯到1999年。

We won't have it happen again until 2037.

下一次再出现要等到2037年。

Astronomer David Chapman explained that this is merely a peculiarity of our calendar; once we stopped doing things based on the moon and started trying to follow the sun and the seasons, we stopped having one reliable full moon per month.

天文学家戴维·查普曼解释说，这不过是历法的一个特殊现象；一旦我们不再依据月亮来安排事务，转而追随太阳和四季，就不再保证每个月都有一次固定的满月了。

The moon cycle is 29.53 days long on average, so in most months we still end up with a single new moon and a single full one.

月相周期平均为29.53天，因此在大多数月份里，我们仍然只有一次新月和一次满月。

But every once in a while, one month steals a full moon from another.

但偶尔也会出现这样的情况：某个月从别的月份那里“偷”走了一次满月。

In 2018 (and in 1999, and again in 2037) both January and March stacked full moons on the first and last nights of the month, leaving February in the dark.

在2018年（以及1999年，还有2037年），一月和三月的月初与月末都恰好各有一次满月，唯独二月没有。

K

Getting two blue moons a year is rare, but we have individual blue moons every few years.

一年出现两次蓝月亮实属罕见，但每隔几年我们就能见到一次单独的蓝月亮。

Also, fun fact: not actually blue.

另外，一个有趣的事实是：它其实并不是蓝色的。

A moon can indeed take on a moody blue color, but this only happens when particles of just the right size disperse through the sky—and it has nothing to do with the moon's status as "blue".

月亮确实可以呈现出忧郁的蓝色，但这只有在大小恰到好处的颗粒散播于天空时才会发生——而且这与月亮被称为“蓝月亮”毫无关系。

Big clouds of ash from volcanic eruptions or fires can do the trick, but it doesn't happen often, and the stars would certainly have to align for two such rare instances to occur at once.

火山爆发或大火产生的大量火山灰可以达到这种效果，但这并不常见，而且要让这两种罕见现象同时发生，简直是天时地利缺一不可。

L

You may have heard that the super special second blue moon of 2018 was also a Paschal moon.

你可能听说过，2018年那个极其特别的第二个蓝月亮同时也是复活节月亮。

This is true!

确实如此！

That just means it was the first full moon of spring, which is often used to determine the date of Easter Sunday.

这仅仅意味着它是春季的第一个满月，而这个满月常被用来确定复活节星期日的日期。

All of this is just calendar nonsense and we refuse to go into it further.

所有这些不过是历法上的文字游戏，我们拒绝进一步深究。

M

Sometimes you'll see a headline that promises a moon with so many qualifiers it makes your head spin.

有时你会看到这样的标题，承诺给你一个修饰语多到让人头晕目眩的月亮。

A super blue blood worm moon, perhaps?

比如，超级蓝血蠕虫月亮？

Or a super blood wolf moon?

还是超级血狼月亮？

Lots of websites will tell you that "wolf moon" is the traditional name of the first full moon of the year in "Native American" cultures, which is kind of a weird thing to claim given that there are 573 registered Tribal Nations in the US alone today, not to mention historically.

许多网站会告诉你，“狼月”是美洲原住民文化中一年中第一次满月的传统名称，这种说法颇为奇怪，因为仅美国境内如今登记在册的部落民族就有573个，更不用说历史上还有更多了。

The idea that hungry, howling wolves were such a universal constant in January that all of North America with its different cultures, geographies, and languages spontaneously came up with the same nickname is illogical.

认为饥饿嚎叫的狼在一月份如此普遍，以至于整个北美大陆上不同文化、不同地理环境、不同语言的人们不约而同地给满月起了同一个绰号，这种想法毫无道理。

N

Many cultures have traditional names for the full moon in a given month or season, so there's quite a list to draw from if you're trying to really juice up a story on a slightly-bigger-than-average view of the moon.

许多文化都为特定月份或季节的满月赋予了传统名称，所以如果你真想把一次略大于平时的月面景观渲染成故事，可供选用的名称相当多。

But these are all based on human calendars and activities and folklore; you will not go outside and see a pink moon in April, though I wish it were so.

但这些名称全都基于人类的历法、活动和民间传说；四月你走到屋外并不会看到一轮粉色的月亮，尽管我倒希望如此。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 1450 词 · 26 句

P1

With population increases and global urbanisation ever accelerating, much attention is focused on the sustainability of our cities and scarce attention is paid to the countryside.

随着人口增长和全球城市化进程不断加快，人们的目光大多聚焦于城市的可持续发展，却鲜少关注乡村。

P2

Rural life is associated with closely-knit communities, sense of belonging, and a simple, tranquil life.

乡村生活让人联想到紧密团结的社区、归属感以及简朴宁静的生活。

Yet whilst some or all of these elements exist in the countryside, so do conservatism and a lack of employment opportunities.

然而，尽管乡村中确实存在上述部分或全部元素，同样存在的还有保守观念和就业机会的匮乏。

And it is mostly due to the latter that rural communities are suffering.

而乡村社区所遭受的困境，主要正是源于后者。

Attracted by the improved economics of urban areas, country-dwellers across the world are moving out, contributing to the excessive crowdedness of densely populated cities, and leaving the countryside in desolation.

受城市地区经济改善的吸引，世界各地的农村居民纷纷外迁，既加剧了人口稠密城市的过度拥挤，也使乡村日渐荒凉。

P3

So how can the countryside ensure its survival among these changes; or should it at all?

那么，在这些变迁之中，乡村该如何确保自身的存续？又或者，它是否真的应当存续下去？

Due to both the distance between residences and facilities and often intermittent public transport, those living in the countryside are heavier automobile users.

由于住所与各类设施之间距离较远，加之公共交通往往时断时续，乡村居民对汽车的依赖程度更高。

Rural dwellers also use more energy maintaining their mostly detached buildings.

乡村居民在维护其以独栋为主的建筑时，消耗的能源也更多。

We may be piled up like boxes in the city, but this brings energy efficiency that the countryside cannot match.

我们在城市里或许像箱子一样被堆叠在一起，但这带来的能源利用效率却是乡村无法比拟的。

Rural energy requirements result in higher carbon emissions per person than in the city, so it is actually irresponsible to endorse such a lifestyle choice.

乡村的能源需求导致人均碳排放高于城市，因此支持这样的生活方式选择实属不负责任。

P4

Protecting the countryside is a hot topic.

保护乡村是一个热门话题。

Those who have made their fortunes in the city often buy second homes in the countryside, visiting intermittently for a sample of a supposedly simple life.

那些在城市里发了财的人，常常在乡村购置第二套住宅，偶尔前来小住，体验一番据称简朴的生活。

The side-effect of this is that house prices are pushed up due to increased demand, leaving them unaffordable for local people.

由此产生的副作用是，需求增加推高了房价，使当地人无力负担。

A solution has yet to be found for this challenge, for market forces are further forcing residents out of the countryside.

对于这一挑战尚无解决之道，因为市场力量正进一步迫使居民离开乡村。

For those who believe in rural preservation, this is deplorable.

对于那些主张乡村保护的人来说，这实在令人痛心。

P5

Whilst an idealised view of the countryside is common, the reality is that rural life has continuously evolved.

虽然人们对乡村的理想化想象很普遍，但现实是乡村生活一直在不断演变。

Industrialisation brought an increase in divisions of labour, changing social links between rural people.

工业化带来了劳动分工的增加，改变了乡村居民之间的社会联系。

The internet has introduced once foreign cultures into the countryside.

互联网将曾经陌生的文化引入了乡村。

Improved travel connections and affordability have made the world more accessible, creating a "global village".

出行条件的改善和交通费用的降低使人们更容易抵达世界各地，造就了一个"地球村"。

Considering these changes, surely an evolving countryside is inevitable.

考虑到这些变化，乡村的不断演变无疑是不可避免的。

P6

Perhaps the countryside should also prepare itself for massive population increases in the future.

或许乡村也应为未来人口的大幅增长做好准备。

With cities across the world growing rapidly, how will the current urban generation respond to the inevitable social changes of mass urbanisation?

随着全球各大城市迅速扩张，当代城市一代将如何应对大规模城市化所带来的必然社会变革？

Will the longing for a simpler life become stronger, fueling a rural renaissance?

对更简单生活的向往是否会变得更加强烈，从而推动一场乡村复兴？

P7

As the world continues to urbanise, the voices calling for rural preservation may well grow louder.

随着世界继续城市化，呼吁保护乡村的声音很可能会越来越响亮。

But change is inevitable.

然而，变化是不可避免的。

We know that the world is going to see new cities emerging from the dust and current cities growing into megacities, but the future of the countryside is less clear.

我们知道，世界将迎来一座座从尘土中崛起的新城市，现有的城市也将发展为超级大城市，但乡村的未来却尚不明朗。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 2446 词 · 27 句

P1

Statements, like "beauty is in the eye of the beholder", are rarely questioned.

“情人眼里出西施”这类说法，很少有人质疑。

They've become so embedded in our consciousness that people think it's absurd to think otherwise.

这些说法已深深植根于我们的意识之中，以至于人们认为持有不同看法是荒谬的。

It might be useful, however, to at least push back on this assumption because people evaluate environment, situations, and people aesthetically.

然而，至少对这一假设提出质疑或许是有益的，因为人们会以审美的方式来评价环境、情境和他人。

We may find that we still believe it is correct, but we may also find that there is a lot more to the situation than you suppose.

我们可能会发现自己仍然认为这一说法是正确的，但我们也可能发现，实际情况远比我们想象的复杂。

P2

A recent study exploring aesthetic taste was published in Cognition.

最近一项关于审美品味的研究发表在《认知》（Cognition）期刊上。

The results from this study show that people agree very much in their aesthetic evaluation of natural objects, but they disagree more about artifacts, or human-made objects.

这项研究的结果表明，人们对自然事物的审美评价高度一致，但对人工制品或人造物体的评价则分歧较大。

P3

The study found that shared taste was most common for faces and natural landscapes, but least common among works of architecture and art.

研究发现，人们对人脸和自然景观的审美品味最为一致，而在建筑和艺术作品方面的一致性最低。

The hypothesis is the commonly pleasing features, like proportion or symmetry, are at work.

该假设认为，诸如比例或对称性等令人愉悦的常见特征发挥了作用。

P4

Preferences for natural scenes might be learned through life experiences.

对自然景观的偏好可能是通过生活经验习得的。

Factors like habitability, safety, and openness might be preferred as people develop.

随着人的成长发展，宜居性、安全性和开阔性等因素可能更受青睐。

Some of the details of landscapes change, but the basics are more common, e.

景观的某些细节各不相同，但基本要素更为普遍，例如

g., water, open spaces, and signs of care.

水、开阔空间和被照料的痕迹。

P5

Art and architecture, unlike natural spaces, do not have the same level of exposure.

与自然空间不同，艺术和建筑的曝光程度并不相同。

So, people do not have the same level of shared taste.

因此，人们的共同审美趣味也并不一致。

P6

It's possible that the lower amount of agreement in the shared taste of artifacts has to do more with elements of style, rather than "behavioral consequences".

人们对人造物的共同审美趣味之所以分歧较大，可能更多与风格元素有关，而非所谓的“行为后果”。

P7

All of the consequences seem reasonable (or at least possible), but I think there might be an interesting philosophical conclusion that they did not draw.

所有这些结论似乎都很合理（或至少有可能成立），但我认为其中或许蕴含着一个他们未曾得出的有趣的哲学结论。

It seems that there is an objective ground to our aesthetic preferences or evaluations.

我们的审美偏好或评价似乎存在客观依据。

I am not saying this would mean beauty is completely objective, just that there could possibly be general objective principles at work, i.

我并不是说这就意味着美是完全客观的，只是说可能确实存在某种普遍的客观原则在起作用，即，

e., beauty is not simply in the eye of the beholder.

美并不仅仅取决于观者的眼光。

P8

Nature exhibits some of the universal aesthetic features, such as radiance, in a common way throughout the world.

自然界以一种普遍的方式，在世界各地展现出某些普遍的审美特征，比如光华感。

It's not exactly the same everywhere, but it is common.

各地的表现并非完全相同，但确是普遍存在的。

For example, a sunset is similar enough in different places to warrant almost universal appeal.

例如，日落在不同地方的景象足够相似，几乎能获得普遍的喜爱。

P9

Possible universal principles of beauty—such as proportion, fittingness, radiance, and others—are general categories, which allow for a wide array of embodiments.

可能存在的普遍美学原则——如比例、适宜性、光华感等——是一些概括性的范畴，可以有多种多样的具体呈现。

When people get involved in making artifacts in architecture or art, they apply these very general concepts in unique ways.

当人们从事建筑或艺术创作时，会以各自独特的方式运用这些非常概括的概念。

But the way they applied the principle may not have universal appeal.

但他们应用这一原则的方式未必具有普遍的吸引力。

P10

This is why it's important to experience diverse cultures and their artifacts because it opens us up to different ways of approaching or constructing beauty.

这就是为什么体验多元文化及其文物艺术很重要，因为它能让我们接触到理解和创造美的不同方式。

And it is always good to question our assumptions.

而且，对我们的固有观念提出质疑总是有益的。

第 3 套 · 4 篇 · 共 2,397 词

Section A · 选词填空（填空已还原）300 词 · 14 句

词库（绿 = 本题所选）：A) applaudB) casualtyC) excludeD) extentE) groupF) hierarchicalG) immediateH) incrediblyI) moderateJ) mortalityK) overtL) praiseworthyM) probeN) scepticallyO) unequal

About 23% of the global population lives in absolute poverty.

全球约有23%的人口生活在绝对贫困之中。

In developing countries there is a low life expectancy, a high infant mortality rate, high levels of unemployment and illiteracy, nutritional levels below acceptable standards and widespread disease with very little or poor quality medical assistance.

发展中国家普遍预期寿命偏低、婴儿死亡率居高不下、失业和文盲率高企、营养水平低于可接受标准，疾病肆虐，而医疗救助极少或质量低劣。

Others live incredibly wealthy and luxurious lives and so the wealth is distributed in a very unequal manner.

另一些人则过着极其富裕奢华的生活，财富的分配因此极为不均。

These are the central problems to questions of charity though charity also includes many other areas such as assisting the elderly.

这些是慈善问题的核心所在，不过慈善还涵盖许多其他领域，比如帮助老年人。

We all applaud when very rich people pledge to give away most of their billions, but they are usually left with millions to pass on, still much more than most people would expect to earn in their lifetime.

当富豪们承诺捐出大部分身家时，我们无不为之喝彩，但他们通常仍留有数百万留给后人，这仍远远超过大多数人一生所能期望挣到的钱。

Even comparatively moderate salaries in the west are very high when compared globally.

即便西方国家中相对中等的收入，放在全球范围内看也相当高。

Someone on £ 20,000 could easily afford to give away £ 2,000 per year and still have plenty to live on plus some luxuries.

一个年收入两万英镑的人，完全可以每年捐出两千英镑，剩下的钱仍足够维持生活并享受一些奢侈品。

Most people agree that giving to charity is morally praiseworthy but charitable behaviour tends to be regarded as above and beyond the call of duty.

大多数人都认同捐赠慈善在道德上值得赞扬，但慈善行为往往被视为超出义务要求的额外之举。

Some argue, however, that charitable behaviour is morally required.

然而，也有人认为慈善行为是道德上的必需。

This means that to fail to behave charitably would be wrong.

这意味着，不行善举即为错误。

The majority of arguments in this vein refer to giving aid to poorer nations but they may also refer to giving time within one's immediate community.

持此类观点的论据大多着眼于向较贫穷国家提供援助，但也可指在自己所在的社区中奉献时间。

Arguments tend to place different requirements on people in different income brackets and exclude entirely people who are merely making ends meet.

这类论据往往对不同收入阶层的人提出不同的要求，而完全将那些仅能勉强维持生计的人排除在外。

Some argue that people should all contribute a certain percentage of their earnings.

有人主张，每个人都应将收入的一定比例捐献出来。

I will group together all those arguments that place a moral requirement on people to give to charity, despite the fact that there is wide disagreement as to the extent of that moral requirement.

尽管人们对于这一道德义务究竟应到何种程度存在很大分歧，我仍会把所有主张人们在道德上有义务向慈善事业捐款的论点归为一类。

Section B · 长篇阅读（信息匹配）1205 词 · 57 句

A

Trade is one of the policy areas where the hostility that exists between populists and classical liberals is most visible.

贸易是民粹主义者与古典自由主义者之间的敌意表现得最为明显的政策领域之一。

Free-traders point to the undeniable good that tariff-free trade has done for consumers across the world and to the observable alleviation of poverty in corners of the world where previously closed markets have been opened up.

自由贸易论者指出，免关税贸易为全球消费者带来了无可否认的益处，而且在那些此前封闭的市场被打开之后，世界某些角落的贫困状况得到了肉眼可见的缓解。

Protectionists point to the domestic producers who've paid the price for this globalized economy in the form of lost livelihoods and hollowed-out communities.

贸易保护主义者则指出，国内生产者为这个全球化经济付出了代价——生计丧失，社区凋敝。

B

The ongoing conservative civil war often degenerates into content-free tribal warfare, but trade is a rare exception.

这场持续不断的保守派内讧往往沦为毫无实质内容的部落式混战，但贸易是一个少有的例外。

There are substantial, thought-out policy proposals on both sides of the argument.

争论双方都有实质性、经过深思熟虑的政策提案。

C

Consequently, trade as a topic of discussion provides an opportunity for liberals and populists to have a real meeting of minds.

因此，贸易作为讨论话题，为自由主义者和民粹主义者提供了一个真正达成思想共识的机会。

Fruitful debates might actually take place in this area, as opposed to the familiar ritual we've become accustomed to of condemnation met with counter-condemnation.

在这个领域，富有成效的辩论或许真能展开，而不只是我们早已习以为常的那套谴责与反谴责的例行仪式。

D

Strange as it might sound, the problem with trade in the modern world isn't a matter of dollars and cents.

听起来或许有些奇怪，当今世界贸易的问题并非关乎金钱得失。

It's a matter of false consciousness.

而是关乎错误意识。

This observation is bound to set Marxist alarm bells ringing in the minds of some readers, but it was first made by Alexis de Tocqueville in 1840.

这一观察必然会令一些读者心中响起马克思主义的警钟，但它最早是由托克维尔在1840年提出的。

E

Tracking the economic development of mankind from primitive to modern societies, Tocqueville observed a paradox unfolding over the centuries as economic realities and human experience of those realities strayed further and further from each other.

托克维尔在追溯人类经济从原始社会到现代社会的发展历程时，发现了一个延续数个世纪的悖论：经济现实与人类对这些现实的体验越来越彼此背离。

In primitive societies, he notes, the division of labor was as yet undeveloped for the most part, requiring each person, family, or tribe to be relatively independent when it came to meeting their own basic material needs.

他指出，在原始社会中，劳动分工在很大程度上尚未发展，每个人、每个家庭或每个部落在满足自身基本物质需求时都相对独立。

Men built their own dwellings, farmed their own land, tended to their own livestock.

人们自己建造住所，耕种自己的土地，照料自家的牲畜。

This is not to deny that basic forms of trade took place, but, for the most part, our primitive ancestors lived fairly self-reliant, if crushingly poor, lives.

这并不是要否认当时已有基本的贸易形式，但在大多数情况下，我们的原始祖先过着相当自给自足的生活，尽管穷得令人窒息。

F

However, the exclusively local and face-to-face nature of economic and political organization in the ancient and prehistoric worlds constantly impressed upon these primitive peoples the uncontrollable reality of others and their needs.

然而，在古代和史前世界，经济与政治组织完全是地方性的、面对面的，这不断使这些原始人真切地意识到，他人及其需求是自己无法控制的现实。

As Tocqueville notes, "as soon as a man begins to deal with common affairs in public, he begins to perceive that he is not so independent of his fellow men as he had first imagined, and that in order to obtain their support he must often lend them his cooperation."

正如托克维尔所指出的："一个人一旦开始在公共场合处理公共事务，就会开始意识到，他并不像最初想象的那样独立于同胞，而为了获得他们的支持，他往往必须与他们合作。"

G

At the advent of the modern world, the division of labor spread further and further throughout society.

随着现代世界的来临，分工在社会中日益广泛地扩展。

Each person became more and more dependent on others for their basic needs.

每个人在基本需求上越来越依赖他人。

And yet, robbed of the engagement with our neighbors and with our local communities that our ancestors were forced into by circumstance, we feel ourselves to be more and more independent of one another.

然而，我们的祖先因环境所迫而不得不与邻里和地方社区打交道，如今我们失去了这种交往，反倒觉得自己越来越不依赖彼此。

As we become more and more dependent on others, we become less and less conscious of our dependence on others.

我们对他人越来越依赖，却越来越意识不到这种依赖。

This is the paradox of trade in the modern world.

这就是现代世界中贸易的悖论。

H

The false consciousness that this paradox generates causes havoc on the debates we have about free trade.

这一悖论所催生的错误意识，严重扰乱了我们关于自由贸易的辩论。

There is scarcely a single commodity in any American household that isn't dependent for its manufacture and sale, through one supply chain or another, on scores of different people spread out across the entire globe.

在任何一个美国家庭中，几乎找不到一件商品，其生产和销售不是通过这样或那样的供应链，依赖于散布在全球各地的数十名不同的人。

But as Tocqueville already foresaw in 1840, we do not feel dependent on these strangers for our way of life.

但正如托克维尔早在1840年就预见的那样，我们并不觉得自己的生活方式依赖于这些陌生人。

No sense of the dependence of our own material welfare on their work ever strikes our national consciousness.

我们的国民意识从未意识到，自身的物质福祉有赖于他人的劳动。

We rarely contemplate the globalized avenues of free trade with gratitude.

我们很少怀着感恩之心去审视自由贸易所开辟的全球化通道。

I

There are two reasons for this.

这背后有两个原因。

The first, to put it bluntly, is money.

第一个原因，直白地说，就是金钱。

Money allows us to purchase the work of others without giving any thought to them as human beings.

金钱让我们得以购买他人的劳动，而无需把他们当作人来考虑。

Unlike our ancestors in their primitive townships, we rarely have to meet face-to-face the people who've invented, built, shipped, or supplied our goods.

不同于生活在原始城镇中的祖先，我们很少需要与那些发明、制造、运输或供应商品的人面对面接触。

No relationship has to be built before an exchange can take place.

在交易发生之前，不必建立任何关系。

Simply agree on a price, and you can have any goods you wish without taking a second thought for the human being involved on the other side of the transaction.

只要商定一个价格，你就能得到任何想要的商品，而不会再多想交易另一端的那个人。

In this way, money makes us feel more independent than we actually are.

如此一来，金钱让我们感觉自己比实际更加独立。

Each of us senses the hold that it has over our fellows.

我们每个人都感受到金钱对他人的掌控力。

We know that if we bid highly enough we can buy ourselves out of the time-consuming labor of building relationships.

我们知道，只要出价足够高，就能花钱让自己免于建立关系这件耗时的苦差。

Money is kind of like magic in that way.

就此而言，金钱有点像魔法。

It gives us a set of rituals to perform and promises that if we do so we'll be able to wield power over others.

它为我们提供了一套需要遵行的仪式，并承诺只要照做，我们就能驾驭他人、掌控他人。

The illusion is created that having enough money to buy something is the equivalent of knowing how to make it yourself.

一种错觉由此产生：有钱买到某样东西，就等于懂得如何亲手把它制造出来。

Gratitude for the anonymous men and women who make up the supply chain rarely makes its way into our consciousness.

对于构成供应链的那些无名男女，我们很少心存感激，也很少意识到他们的存在。

J

Anonymity, in fact, is the second root cause of the free-trade paradox.

事实上，匿名性是自由贸易悖论的第二个根本原因。

Modernity has emancipated everyone from the limits of location and community.

现代性把每个人从地域与社群的局限中解放了出来。

By and large, when we trade, we trade with strangers; when we vote, we vote for strangers; when we watch, read, or listen to stories, the tellers of the tales are strangers.

总体而言，我们交易时，交易的对象是陌生人；投票时，投票的对象是陌生人；观看、阅读或聆听故事时，讲故事的人同样是陌生人。

As opposed to the ancestors Tocqueville compares us to, we do not know the people with whom we have to do, in either the economic or the political sphere.

与托克维尔用来和我们作比较的先辈不同，无论在经济领域还是政治领域，我们都不认识那些与我们打交道的人。

This is simply the shadow side of the miracle of markets, which, for the first time in history, have allowed strangers to look after each other.

这不过是市场奇迹的阴暗面；正是市场，在历史上第一次让陌生人得以彼此关照。

They've also allowed each of us to live more and more of our lives exclusively as strangers to other people.

然而，市场也让我们每个人越来越多地只是以陌生人的身份度过自己的生活。

This is how Tocqueville—rather pessimistically—describes us: Each, standing apart, is like a stranger to the destiny of others; his children and personal friends forming for him the entire human race.

托克维尔颇为悲观地这样描述我们：每个人各自独立，对他人的命运如同陌生人；他的子女和私交好友便构成了他眼中全部的人类。

As for the remainder of his fellow citizens, he is beside them, but he does not see them.

至于其余的同胞公民，他虽与他们比肩而立，却看不见他们。

He touches them, but he does not feel them.

他触碰到他们，却感觉不到他们的存在。

He exists only in and for himself.

他只为自身而存在，也仅存在于自身之中。

K

The last sentence but one is as apt a summary as one could hope to come by of how each of us functions in the modern economy: "He touches them, but he does not feel them."

倒数第二句话是对我们每个人在现代经济中如何运作的最佳概括，正如人们所能期待的那样恰如其分："他触碰了它们，却感受不到它们。"

L

This is the greatest challenge facing defenders of free trade.

这是自由贸易捍卫者面临的最大挑战。

It's exceedingly difficult for human beings to feel gratitude toward strangers, and the global marketplace that has made us so rich has also made us strangers to one another.

人类极难对陌生人怀有感激之情，而使我们变得如此富足的全球市场，同时也让我们彼此形同陌路。

Our brains are hardwired for tribal life, and tribes do not take kindly to strangers.

我们的大脑为部落生活而生，而部落对陌生人向来并不友善。

Impressing a sense of dependence upon and gratitude toward foreign strangers is therefore an uphill task.

因此，让人们对外国陌生人产生依赖感和感激之情，是一项极其艰难的任务。

M

If free-traders are going to win policy arguments in the future, they'll have to find a way of forging bonds of affection between American consumers and foreign producers.

如果自由贸易者希望在未来赢得政策辩论，他们就必须找到一种方式，在美国消费者与外国生产者之间建立起情感纽带。

Only by de-anonymizing the men and women who supply us with the goods and services we enjoy from overseas and by creating a sense of solidarity and relationship across borders that transcends economic interest can free trade win the day.

只有将那些为我们提供海外商品和服务的男男女女从匿名状态中还原出来，并在跨国界层面建立起一种超越经济利益的团结感和联系，自由贸易才能赢得胜利。

Otherwise, the inborn biological upper hand that protectionists have in the form of nationalist solidarity is bound to win the day at the ballot box.

否则，保护主义者凭借民族主义团结这一先天的生物学优势，势必会在投票箱前赢得最终胜利。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 1444 词 · 24 句

P1

There are hundreds of personality quizzes online that assert they can ascertain whether the right or left half of your brain is dominant.

网上有数百种人格测试，声称能够判定你是右脑还是左脑占主导。

Left-brained people are supposedly logical and excel at language and math while right-brained people are more imaginative, emotionally intelligent and skilled with spatial reasoning.

据称，左脑型人擅长逻辑，在语言和数学方面表现出色，而右脑型人更具想象力、情商更高，空间推理能力也更强。

There's just one problem: That's not how brains work.

但问题只有一个：大脑并非如此运作。

P2

Popular science enthusiasts sort of took this idea and ran with it, and it's become woven in popular culture now, and it's not going away.

科普爱好者多少把这个观点奉为圭臬并加以传播，如今它已融入流行文化，而且不会销声匿迹。

P3

Despite this enduring belief, there's no such thing as being "right-brained" or "left-brained".

尽管这种观念根深蒂固，但人根本不存在所谓“右脑型”或“左脑型”。

Whether you're someone who tends more towards creativity or logic has nothing to do with one hemisphere of your brain being dominant over the other.

一个人更偏重创造力还是逻辑思维，与大脑某个半球是否占主导毫无关系。

But the actual science of how the two halves of our brains work together is sometimes stranger than fiction.

然而，关于大脑两半球如何协同工作的真实科学，有时比虚构的故事还要离奇。

P4

The human brain is divided into two hemispheres, the left and right.

人脑分为左右两个半球。

In all vertebrate animals, the right hemisphere controls the left side of the body and vice versa.

在所有脊椎动物中，右半球控制身体左侧，左半球控制身体右侧。

And scientists have long known, thanks to the behaviors of patients who suffered brain injuries, that different areas of the brain do different things.

科学家早就知道，大脑的不同区域各司其职，这一点是从脑损伤患者的行为表现中获知的。

P5

But many scientists struggled with this idea, because the very suggestion that the left and right halves of the brain operate differently disrupted the idea that nature tends toward perfect symmetry.

但许多科学家曾对这一观点感到困惑，因为只要承认大脑左右两半运作方式不同，就动摇了自然界趋向完美对称这一观念。

P6

Work by neuroscientists has revealed the importance of different hemispheres of the brain for different activities.

神经科学家的研究揭示了大脑不同半球在不同活动中的重要作用。

However, their research quickly saw some misinterpretations in the general public: Some presumed creative people must be right-brained and logical people left-brained.

然而，他们的研究很快便发现公众中存在一些误解：有人认为，有创造力的人必定是右脑型，而讲逻辑的人则是左脑型。

It is proven that not only is personality unrelated to the different halves of the brain, but people aren't really right- or left-brained to begin with.

事实已证明，性格不仅与大脑左右半球无关，而且人们原本就并非真正的右脑型或左脑型。

The idea that we have left-dominant people and right-dominant people, and that this is related to personality, is categorically false.

所谓存在左脑主导型和右脑主导型的人，且这与性格相关，这种说法完全是错误的。

That's never been supported in the neuroscience community.

这一说法从未得到神经科学界的认可。

Neuroscientists don't believe that and never have.

神经科学家从不相信这一点，过去也从未相信过。

P7

What scientists learned is that there are really important differences between the left hemisphere and the right hemisphere.

科学家所了解到的是，大脑左半球和右半球之间确实存在重要差异。

It's just that they have nothing to do with personality or whether cognitive strategy is more logical or free spirited or creative.

只不过这些差异与性格无关，也与认知策略是更偏向逻辑、更自由随性还是更具创造力无关。

While researchers have shown the limitations of how the hemispheres of our brains influence our lives, it's not difficult to understand the appeal of such ideas.

虽然研究人员已经揭示了大脑两半球对人们生活影响的局限性，但这类观点为何如此具有吸引力，也并不难理解。

People are endlessly fascinated by themselves and their friends, and the subtle differences in how people think about the world are really meaningful to them.

人们对自己和朋友有着无穷的好奇，而人们思考世界的细微差异对他们而言确实意义重大。

When you come up with an online quiz that tells us something about ourselves, we're drawn to that.

当你推出一个能让我们更了解自己的在线测试时，我们就会被它吸引。

It's irresistible.

这是难以抗拒的。

But you have to take it with an enormous grain of salt.

但你必须对此抱以极大的怀疑态度。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 2448 词 · 22 句

P1

One hundred thirty-five students, four teachers, one giant classroom: This is what 9th grade looks like at Westwood High School, in Mesa, Arizona's largest school system.

135名学生、4位老师、一间巨型教室：这就是亚利桑那州最大校区梅萨市韦斯特伍德高中九年级的日常景象。

There, an innovative teaching model has taken hold, and is spreading to other schools in the district and beyond.

在那里，一种创新教学模式已经落地生根，并正向该学区及其他地区的学校推广开来。

P2

Five years ago, faced with high teacher turnover and declining student enrollment, Westwood's leaders decided to try something different.

五年前，面对教师流失严重和学生人数下滑的困境，韦斯特伍德的校领导决定另辟蹊径。

Working with professors at Arizona State University's teachers college, they piloted a classroom model known as team teaching, which allows teachers to dissolve the walls that separate their classes across physical or grade divides.

他们与亚利桑那州立大学教育学院的教授合作，试点了一种被称为协同教学的课堂模式，让教师们打破班级之间的壁垒，不再受学科或年级界限的束缚。

P3

The teachers share large groups of students—sometimes 100 or more—and rotate between group instruction, one-on-one interventions, small study groups, or whatever the teachers as a team agree is a priority that day.

教师们共同负责大批学生——有时多达100人甚至更多——并在集体授课、一对一辅导、小组研讨等方式之间轮换，具体安排由教师团队根据当天的重点共同商定。

What looks at times like chaos is in fact a carefully orchestrated plan: Each morning, the Westwood teams meet to hammer out a personalized program for every student the team will focus on that day.

乍看之下有时像一团混乱，实则是精心编排的计划：每天早晨，韦斯特伍德的各教师团队都会开会，为当天需要重点关注的每一位学生制定个性化方案。

P4

By giving teachers more opportunity to collaborate, Mesa's administrators hoped to fill staffing gaps and boost teacher morale and retention.

通过给教师提供更多协作机会，梅萨的管理者希望以此填补师资缺口，提升教师士气并留住人才。

Initial research suggests the gamble could pay off.

初步研究显示，这一大胆尝试可能正在见效。

P5

"Teachers are doing fantastic things, but it's very rare a teacher walks into another room to see what's happening," said Andi Fourlis, superintendent of Mesa Public Schools.

"老师们在做着了不起的事，但一位老师走进另一间教室看看别人在做什么，这种情况非常少见，"梅萨公立学校学监安迪·福利斯说道。

"Our profession is so slow to advance because we are working in isolation."

"我们这个行业之所以进步如此缓慢，就是因为我们各自孤立地工作。"

P6

Of course, overhauling teaching approaches can't fix all the frustrations teachers have, such as low pay, but early results from Mesa show team teaching may be helping to reverse low morale.

当然，改革教学方式无法解决教师面临的所有困扰，比如薪酬偏低等问题，但梅萨的早期成果表明，协同教学可能有助于扭转士气低落的局面。

In a survey of hundreds of the district's teachers, researchers found those who worked on teams reported greater job satisfaction, more frequent collaborations with colleagues, and more positive interactions with students.

在对学区数百名教师的调查中，研究人员发现，参与团队教学的教师工作满意度更高、与同事协作更频繁、与学生的互动也更为积极。

P7

Another benefit of teams, teachers say, is that they can help each other improve their instruction.

教师们说，团队的另一个好处是成员可以互相帮助提升教学水平。

During one planning session, English teacher Jeff Hall shared a performance appraisal with a science teacher: Her recent lecture on something she called "the central dogma of biology" had bewildered him and their other teammates.

在一次集体备课中，英语教师杰夫·霍尔向一位科学教师展示了一份教学评估：她最近讲授的关于她所谓的“生物学中心法则”的课程令他和团队其他成员感到困惑。

P8

"If the science is too confusing for me, can you imagine the frustration you feel as kids?"

“如果这门科学对我而言都太难理解，你们可以想象孩子们会有多沮丧。”

Hall said.

霍尔说道。

But the science teacher, he said, wouldn't have known about the confusion on her own.

但他说，那位科学教师自己并没有意识到这种困惑。

P9

The model is not for everyone.

这种模式并不适合所有人。

Some teachers approached about volunteering for a team have said they prefer to work alone.

一些被邀请自愿加入团队的教师表示，他们更喜欢独立工作。

Team teaching can also be a scheduling nightmare, especially at schools like Westwood where only some staff work in teams.

协同教学在排课上也可能是一场噩梦，尤其是在像韦斯特伍德这样只有部分教职员工参与团队合作的学校。

There are also thorny questions like how to evaluate four teachers on the performance of 135 students.

还有一些棘手的问题，比如如何根据135名学生的表现来评价四位教师的工作。

But for the time being, it seems to be working.

但就目前而言，这似乎行之有效。

2025年6月

第 1 套 · 4 篇 · 共 2,408 词

Section A · 选词填空（填空已还原）298 词 · 15 句

词库（绿 = 本题所选）：A) aestheticB) chronicC) contaminatedD) conventionallyE) emissionsF) environmentG) hamperingH) incidentallyI) infringementJ) intriguedK) juvenileL) outletM) rotatingN) vibrationsO) weeding

In her second year, Charlene Duong learned of the use of poisonous, synthetic pesticides on her college campus.

大学二年级时，陈良（Charlene Duong）得知学校校园内使用了有毒的合成农药。

Shocked but not surprised, she knew she had to do something.

震惊之余，她并不意外，她知道自己必须做点什么。

Along with a couple of classmates, Charlene did a quick web search and discovered a small but growing movement led by the organization, Herbicide-Free Campus (HFC), to rid college campuses of artificial herbicides.

陈良和几个同学一起迅速在网上搜索，发现了一个规模不大但正在壮大的运动，由"无除草剂校园"（Herbicide-Free Campus，简称HFC）这一组织发起，旨在让大学校园彻底摆脱人工合成除草剂。

They were intrigued.

他们很感兴趣。

Like many, Charlene experiences climate anxiety—a chronic fear of a climate catastrophe—and was, at the time, looking for an outlet.

和许多人一样，陈良也饱受气候焦虑之苦——即对气候灾难的长期恐惧——当时她正想找一个宣泄的出口。

When she discovered the HFC movement, she said she felt she "had found a specific area to focus on that still fit into the larger picture of fighting for a healthier, safer, cleaner environment for all."

她说，当她发现HFC运动时，感觉自己"找到了一个具体的着力点，同时又能融入为所有人争取更健康、更安全、更清洁环境这一更大的事业之中"。

Toxic herbicide use in university land care is not unique.

在大学校园绿地养护中使用有毒除草剂并非个例。

Most institutions of higher education rely on synthetic pesticides and fertilizers to achieve aesthetic goals.

大多数高等院校都依赖合成农药和化肥来达到美化校园的目的。

Having a "beautiful" campus means green and perfectly maintained lawns along with flower beds and paved sidewalks.

拥有一座"美丽"的校园，意味着草坪绿意盎然、修剪得整整齐齐，配以花坛和铺设平整的人行道。

But these conventionally managed campuses can come at a cost: increased cancer risk, contaminated waterways, poisoned wildlife and lifeless soil.

但这些采用常规方式管理的校园是要付出代价的：患癌风险上升、水道污染、野生动物中毒以及土壤丧失生机。

Pesticide use on college campuses also contributes to our global climate crisis.

大学校园使用农药也加剧了全球气候危机。

The use of chemicals to get rid of insects or unwanted plant life can increase indirect emissions as they can include petroleum-based ingredients.

使用化学药剂消灭害虫或清除不需要的植物，会增加间接排放，因为这些药剂可能含有石油基成分。

Pesticide use also decreases the life in soil, hampering the ability of soils to absorb carbon or retain water and thus reducing campuses' ability to recover quickly from climate-related extreme weather events like droughts and floods.

农药的使用还会减少土壤中的生物，削弱土壤吸收碳和保持水分的能力，从而降低校园快速应对干旱、洪涝等气候相关极端天气事件的能力。

Instead of using toxic chemicals, students working with HFC help out with weeding the campus grounds.

HFC 的学生志愿者不用有毒化学品，而是帮助清理校园里的杂草。

"This work reminds me to be in the present moment as I play my role in reducing herbicide use and keeping my campus safe and healthy," says Charlene.

查琳说："这项工作提醒我要专注于当下，在减少除草剂使用、维护校园安全与健康的过程中尽自己的一份力。"

Section B · 长篇阅读（信息匹配）1217 词 · 49 句

A

Librarians know the value of their community services, and their patrons appreciate their importance as well.

图书馆员深知社区服务的价值，而读者也充分认可其重要性。

But in an increasingly digital world, we see the role of libraries as community and cultural centers at times undervalued.

但在一个日益数字化的世界里，我们发现图书馆作为社区和文化中心的作用有时被低估了。

When shrinking municipal budgets combine with the nonstop technological revolution, public library services that focus on building communities face-to-face, inspiring and educating patrons about art, literature, and music, and helping patrons engage in civil discourse can seem old-fashioned.

当不断缩减的市政预算与无休止的技术革命交织在一起时，那些致力于面对面建设社区、以艺术、文学和音乐启迪和教育读者、并帮助读者参与公民对话的公共图书馆服务，可能会显得有些过时。

But it is precisely those shrinking budgets and the assault of technologically mediated life that make public libraries' cultural and community offerings more important than ever.

然而，恰恰是这些不断缩减的预算和科技化生活的冲击，使得公共图书馆的文化与社区服务比以往任何时候都更加重要。

B

Many people point out the value public libraries bring to their communities.

许多人指出了公共图书馆为其社区带来的价值。

More than just books and banks of computers, libraries are still places where individuals gather to explore, interact, and imagine.

图书馆远不止是书籍和成排电脑的集合，它们仍然是人们汇聚一堂、探索、交流和激发想象力的场所。

Some of the specific ways in which libraries add value to our communities and serve as cultural centers for our patrons are community builders, centers for the arts, and champions of youth.

图书馆为我们社区增值、为读者充当文化中心的一些具体方式包括：建设社区、充当艺术中心、以及支持青少年成长。

Libraries serve in these capacities and are more than just about information.

图书馆在这些方面发挥着作用，其意义远不止于信息的传递。

C

As community builders libraries are engaged in incredible work.

作为社区建设者，图书馆正在从事令人赞叹的工作。

From tiny public libraries to huge city institutions with multiple branches, libraries across the United States are building community and supporting local culture in exciting ways.

从规模很小的公共图书馆到拥有多个分馆的大型城市图书馆，全美各地的图书馆正以令人振奋的方式建设社区、支持本地文化。

These are inspiring and hopefully will encourage librarians interested in community services and cultural outreach to make connections with each other, share ideas, and build partnerships.

这些事例令人鼓舞，有望激励那些对社区服务和文化推广感兴趣的图书馆员相互联系、分享理念、建立合作关系。

Supporters of libraries as community builders claim that unless you are out there changing neighborhoods, you are not completing the work you are to do.

将图书馆视为社区建设者的支持者们声称：除非你切实走出馆外、改变社区面貌，否则你就没有完成自己应尽的工作。

Strengthening neighborhoods and championing the cultural lives of communities are big responsibilities.

加强社区建设、推动社区文化生活，是重大的责任。

D

Place-based economic development stresses the importance of offering attractive, functional, and community-based places, such as libraries, in town squares and depressed neighborhoods.

以地域为基础的经济发展强调在城镇广场和衰落社区中提供有吸引力、功能完善且扎根社区的场所，如图书馆。

Like a major department store in a mall, libraries attract large numbers of people, creating economic opportunities for numerous businesses and organizations in the surrounding area.

图书馆就像购物中心里的大型百货商场，能够吸引大量人流，为周边地区的众多企业和组织创造经济机遇。

Large cities, medium-sized ones, and even small towns have successfully transformed their libraries into the hubs of vibrant neighborhoods.

大城市、中等城市乃至小城镇，都成功地将图书馆转变为充满活力的社区中心。

E

As key municipal agencies, and focal points for community education, libraries are major players in creating livable, environmentally friendly cities and towns.

图书馆作为重要的市政机构和社区教育的核心，是打造宜居、环保型城乡的重要力量。

The Urban Libraries Council released a report detailing the unique ways in which libraries can further sustainability at the local level.

城市图书馆协会发布了一份报告，详细阐述了图书馆在地方层面推进可持续发展的独特方式。

Beyond ensuring that library construction projects consider environmental impact, libraries can take a lead in supporting local foods and craftsmen, like the Peabody Institute Library's partnering with local businesses to pioneer a farmers' market in their courtyard, or the Richmond Public Library's seed lending library which "nurtures locally-adapted plant varieties, and fosters community resilience, self-reliance and a culture of sharing."

除了确保图书馆建设项目考虑环境影响外，图书馆还可以带头支持本地食品和手工艺人，例如皮博迪学院图书馆与当地企业合作在庭院里率先开设农贸市场，或者里士满公共图书馆的种子借阅项目，该项目"培育适应当地环境的植物品种，增强社区的韧性、自力更生能力和分享文化"。

F

Archives preserve historic artifacts, oral histories, digital history projects, and scholarly writings relevant to the community, including minority groups.

档案馆保存着与社区（包括少数族裔群体）相关的历史文物、口述史、数字历史项目和学术著作。

Communities lucky enough to have archivists have a great advantage when it comes to organizing historical records and artifacts.

拥有档案管理员的社区在整理历史记录和文物方面拥有很大的优势。

An organized archive is a place where people can research their ancestry and immigration history, do environmental research, and more.

管理有序的档案馆是人们研究家族血统与移民历史、开展环境研究等的场所。

An archivist is an advocate for preservation who, among other things, coordinates the restoration of maps and paintings, the digitization of vital records, and the creation of oral history projects.

档案管理员是文化遗产的守护者，其职责之一是协调地图和绘画的修复、重要记录的数字化以及口述史项目的创建等工作。

With projects like the Massachussets Memories Road Show and the Veterans History Project, evidence of the importance of archives is everywhere.

随着"马萨诸塞记忆巡回展"和"退伍军人历史项目"等项目的开展，档案重要性的证据随处可见。

G

In the words of Robert Putnam, "People may go to the library looking mainly for information, but they find each other there."

用罗伯特·帕特南的话来说：“人们去图书馆也许主要为查找资料，却在那里遇见了彼此。”

New moms connect at baby story-times; elderly people, often facing difficult life transitions, attend events and find that they make new friends; teenagers meet up in libraries' teen spaces after school; and readers discuss current events in the periodicals room.

新手妈妈们在婴儿故事时间里结识；老年人常面临艰难的人生过渡期，他们参加活动并结交了新朋友；青少年放学后聚集在图书馆的青少年专区；读者们则在期刊阅览室里讨论时事。

In libraries, community-building connections are happening all the time.

在图书馆里，社区纽带的建立无时无刻不在发生。

H

As Keith Richards said, "The public library is the great equalizer."

正如基思·理查兹所言：“公共图书馆是最伟大的公平器。”

Despite the rising costs of concert and theater tickets, public library events (including concerts, author visits, and gallery displays) are often offered free of charge, enabling people of any income level to attend.

尽管音乐会和戏剧门票价格不断上涨，公共图书馆的活动（包括音乐会、作家见面会和画廊展览）往往免费开放，使各个收入水平的人都能参加。

In addition, library book groups allow people to explore and discuss the literary arts, and the Great Stories Club introduces at-risk youth to literature.

此外，图书馆的读书小组让人们得以探讨和交流文学艺术，而“伟大故事俱乐部”则引导处于风险中的青少年走近文学。

The best part: it's all free and open to the public.

最棒的是：这一切都是免费的，并且向公众开放。

I

In a time when education is increasingly expensive, public libraries provide information and educational opportunities free for all people, regardless of their socio-economic status.

在这个教育日益昂贵的时代，公共图书馆不分社会经济地位，向所有人免费提供信息和教育机会。

Offered by libraries across the country, American Library Association's Let's Talk about It programs are wonderful examples of scholar-facilitated learning opportunities in libraries.

美国图书馆协会的“让我们谈谈”项目由全国各地的图书馆承办，是图书馆中学者引导型学习机会的优秀范例。

In addition, many libraries present classes and discussion programs, and some even provide online continuing education courses such as the Universal Class database.

此外，许多图书馆开设课程和讨论项目，有些图书馆甚至提供在线继续教育课程，例如“通用课堂”数据库。

J

Librarians know that patrons aren't just information consumers, they're information producers.

图书馆员知道，读者不仅仅是信息的消费者，更是信息的创造者。

Patrons use the library to gain knowledge in order to create their own new and independent works.

读者利用图书馆获取知识，以便创作出属于自己的全新独立作品。

Increasing numbers of libraries provide spaces and services that meet the needs of people who want to learn how to edit Wikipedia, set up blogs or podcasts, create their own magazines, and so much more.

越来越多的图书馆提供符合人们需求的空间和服务，帮助他们学习如何编辑维基百科、开设博客或播客、创办自己的杂志，乃至更多。

Many libraries offer art or writing workshops and groups, and some provide music practice rooms for patrons.

许多图书馆开设艺术或写作工作坊和兴趣小组，有些还为读者提供音乐练习室。

Programs like ImaginOn in Charlotte, North Carolina, provide exciting models that take community partnership, creativity, and creation to a new level.

北卡罗来纳州夏洛特市的ImaginOn（想象空间）等项目提供了令人振奋的范例，将社区合作、创造力和创作提升到了新的高度。

K

The decline of civil discourse stems in part from the fact that it is so easy for people to watch news about, buy products from, and engage—in both the virtual and real worlds—only with those of similar backgrounds and ideologies.

文明交流的式微，部分源于这样一个事实：无论是在虚拟世界还是现实世界中，人们都很容易只接触与自己背景和意识形态相似的人——只看这些人播报的新闻、只购买这些人的产品、只与这些人交往。

Public libraries, through such programs as The Human Library and Socrates Café, can help build small communities of difference that encourage people to interact with and learn from each other through dialogue.

公共图书馆通过"真人图书馆"（The Human Library）和"苏格拉底咖啡馆"（Socrates Café）等项目，可以帮助构建由差异人群组成的小型社群，鼓励人们通过对话相互交流、彼此学习。

By both actively promoting civil discourse through these programs, and modeling and upholding the principles of free inquiry and expression for all, libraries help individuals rediscover the importance of and increased need for civil discourse in American life.

图书馆一方面通过这些项目积极推动文明交流，另一方面以身作则、维护面向所有人的自由探究与表达原则，从而帮助个人重新认识到文明交流在美国生活中的重要性及其日益增长的必要性。

L

Free tutoring, homework help programs, and summer reading programs for kids and teens help bridge the economic divide that impacts students' academic performance.

面向儿童和青少年的免费辅导、作业辅导和暑期阅读项目，有助于弥合影响学生学业表现的经济鸿沟。

The cost of hiring a private tutor is well beyond what many library patrons can afford, so libraries offer homework help and tutoring online, by phone, in person, and even through social media and homework apps.

聘请私人教师的费用远远超出许多图书馆读者的承受能力，因此图书馆通过线上、电话、面对面，甚至社交媒体和作业辅导应用等方式提供作业辅导和教学服务。

Annual summer reading programs also have a positive impact on student performance and, according to a 2010 study conducted by Dominican University's Graduate School of Library and Information Science, students' reading skills get a boost from these popular nationwide events.

一年一度的暑期阅读项目同样对学生的学业表现有积极影响。根据多米尼加大学图书馆与信息科学研究生院2010年开展的一项研究，这些广受欢迎的全国性活动提升了学生的阅读技能。

M

Through library collections, programs, and physical spaces, children learn to share, to be engaged in their communities, to participate in the arts, and to explore their immediate world and the world at large.

通过馆藏资源、各类项目和实体空间，孩子们学会了分享、融入社区、参与艺术活动，并探索身边的世界和更广阔的天地。

There are surely endless examples of innovative library services for children, including the Middle Country Public Library's Nature Explorium, which engages children in learning about the natural world.

面向儿童的创新图书馆服务无疑不胜枚举，比如中乡村公共图书馆（Middle Country Public Library）的"自然探索园"（Nature Explorium），引导孩子们学习有关自然世界的知识。

N

These examples are just a few of the many amazing things that public libraries around the United States (and the world) are doing to build and maintain strong community connections.

这些例子只是美国（乃至全世界）各地公共图书馆为建立和维系牢固的社区纽带所做的诸多出色工作中的一小部分。

We encourage you to try some of these ideas in your own libraries, and we hope that these ideas will help you be better able to convince your community leaders of the important role that public libraries play in communities large and small.

我们鼓励各位在自己的图书馆中尝试其中一些理念，也希望这些理念能帮助您更有说服力地让社区领导者认识到公共图书馆在大大小小的社区中所发挥的重要作用。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 1443 词 · 25 句

P1

Nationally, one in six children miss 15 or more days of school in a year.

从全国范围看，每六个孩子中就有一个一年缺课15天或更多。

Education officials have deplored all this missed instruction.

教育官员对这些错失的教学时间深感痛惜。

P2

These chronically absent students suffer academically because of all the classroom instruction they miss out on.

这些长期缺课的学生因错失了大量课堂教学而在学业上遭受损失。

In 2015, the U.S. secretary of education responded to this crisis, urging communities to support every student to attend every day and be successful in school.

2015年，美国教育部长对这一危机作出回应，敦促各社区支持每一名学生每天到校并顺利完成学业。

His open letter stated that missing 10% of school days in a year for any reason—excused or unexcused—is a primary cause of low academic achievement.

他在公开信中指出，无论出于何种原因——无论是请假还是未请假——一年内缺课天数达到10%，是学业成绩低下的首要原因。

P3

Worrying about whether children attend school makes sense.

担心孩子是否到校上课，这种心情合乎情理。

After all, if students don't show up, teachers can't teach them.

毕竟，学生不到校，老师就无法教他们。

P4

But what if America's attendance crisis is about much more than students missing class?

但美国的出勤危机，会不会远不止学生缺课那么简单？

What if, instead, it is a reflection of family and community crises these students face—such as being ejected from the family apartment, fearing for their safety in their neighborhood or suffering an illness?

它是否反而是这些学生所面临的家庭与社区危机的写照——例如被赶出家门、在所住街区为自身安全担忧，或遭受疾病困扰？

P5

As social scientists we investigated how excused and unexcused absences relate to children's academic achievement.

作为社会科学研究者，我们考察了请假缺课与无故缺课同儿童学业成绩之间的关系。

P6

We find that absences excused by a parent do little to harm children's learning.

我们发现，经家长请假的缺课几乎不会损害儿童的学习。

In fact, children with no unexcused absences—but 15 to 18 excused absences—have test scores equal to their peers who have no absences.

事实上，那些没有无故缺课、但有15至18次请假缺课的儿童，其考试成绩与从不缺课的同龄人相当。

P7

Meanwhile, the average child with even just one unexcused absence does much worse academically than peers with none.

与此同时，平均而言，哪怕只旷课一次（无故缺勤）的孩子，其学业表现也远逊于从未缺勤的同龄人。

P8

We believe unexcused absence is a strong signal of the many challenges children and families face, including economic and medical hardships.

我们认为，无故缺勤是一个强烈信号，反映出儿童及其家庭所面临的种种困境，包括经济和医疗方面的艰难处境。

Unexcused absences can be a powerful signal of how those out-of-school challenges affect children's academic progress.

无故缺勤可以有力地揭示校外的这些困境如何影响儿童的学业进步。

P9

Our evidence suggests unexcused absences are problematic, but for a different reason than people often think.

我们的证据表明，无故缺勤确实是个问题，但其原因与人们通常所想的不同。

Absence from school, and especially unexcused absence, matters mainly as a signal of many crises children and their families may be facing.

缺课，尤其是无故缺勤，其意义主要在于它是一个信号，表明儿童及其家庭可能正面临多重危机。

It matters less as a cause of lower student achievement due to missed instruction.

它作为缺课导致教学内容缺失、进而拉低学业成绩的原因，其重要性要小得多。

P10

How we choose to think of school absences matters for educational policy.

我们如何看待学生缺勤，对教育政策的制定至关重要。

School attendance policies typically hold schools and families accountable for the days children miss, regardless of whether they were excused or unexcused absences.

学校的出勤政策通常要求学校和家庭对孩子缺勤的天数负责，而不论这些缺勤是有正当理由还是无故缺勤。

P11

These policies assume that missing school for any reason harms children academically because they are missing classroom instruction.

这些政策假定，无论出于何种原因缺课都会因错过课堂教学而损害儿童的学业。

They also assume that schools will be able to effectively intervene by reducing student absences.

它们还假定，学校能够通过减少学生缺勤来进行有效干预。

We find neither to be the case.

我们发现，这两种假设都不成立。

P12

As a result, these attendance policies end up disproportionately punishing families dealing with out-of-school crises in their lives and pressuring schools who serve them to get students to school more often.

结果是，这些出勤政策不成比例地惩罚了那些正应对生活中校外危机的家庭，同时给服务于这些家庭的学校施加压力，迫使它们让学生更频繁地到校。

P13

We instead suggest using unexcused absence from school as a signal to channel resources to the children and families who need them most.

我们则建议将无故缺课作为一种信号，借此把资源投向最需要帮助的儿童及其家庭。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 2450 词 · 23 句

P1

After earning a bachelor's degree, I was determined to do what I love.

获得学士学位后，我决心从事自己热爱的事业。

I headed straight to graduate school to investigate the social problems that fascinated me.

我直接进入研究生院，研究那些令我着迷的社会问题。

P2

For almost a decade, I told everyone I encountered that they should do the same.

近十年来，我一直告诉遇到的每个人，他们也应该这样做。

"Follow your passion," I counseled.

我劝告别人：“追随你的热爱。”

"You can figure out the employment stuff later."

“至于就业方面的事，以后再想办法解决。”

P3

It wasn't until I began to research this widely accepted career advice that I understood how problematic it really was.

直到我开始研究这一广为接受的职业建议时，我才明白它究竟有多大问题。

P4

As a sociologist, I interviewed college students and professional workers to learn what it really meant to pursue their dreams, which I will refer to here as the passion principle.

作为一名社会学家，我采访了大学生和职场人士，以了解追求梦想对他们究竟意味着什么——我在本文中将其称为“热爱原则”。

I was stunned by what I found out about this principle in the research for my new book.

在我的新书研究过程中，我所了解到的关于这一原则的情况令我大为震惊。

P5

Surveys show the American public has long held the passion principle in high regard as a career decision-making priority.

调查显示，美国公众长期以来一直将“热爱原则”视为职业决策的首要考量。

And its popularity is even stronger among those facing job instability.

而在面临工作不稳定的人群中，这一原则更为流行。

P6

Advocates of the passion principle found it compelling because they believed that following one's passion can provide workers with both the motivation necessary to work hard and a place to find fulfillment.

“热爱原则”的支持者认为它很有说服力，因为他们相信，追随热爱既能为劳动者提供努力工作所需的动机，也能为他们找到成就感提供一个方向。

P7

Yet, what I found is that following one's passion does not necessarily lead to fulfillment, but is one of the most powerful cultural forces perpetuating overwork.

然而，我发现，追随热爱并不一定会带来成就感，反而是在让过度工作长期延续的最强大的文化力量之一。

I also found that promoting the pursuit of one's passion helps perpetuate social inequalities due to the fact that not everyone has the same economic resources to allow them to pursue their passion with ease.

我还发现，倡导追随个人激情的做法会加剧社会不平等，因为并非人人都拥有同样的经济资源，能够从容地追求自己的激情所在。

P8

While the passion principle is broadly popular, not everyone has the necessary resources to turn their passion into a stable, good-paying job.

尽管“激情原则”广受欢迎，但并非人人都有必要的资源，能把激情转化为一份稳定且收入可观的工作。

Passion-seekers from wealthy families are better able to wait until a job they are passionate about comes along without worrying about student loans in the meantime.

出身富裕家庭的追梦者更有条件等待心仪工作的出现，而无须在此期间为助学贷款发愁。

They are also better situated to take unpaid internships to get their foot in the door while their parents pay their rent.

他们也更有条件接受无薪实习以跻身行业，而房租则由父母承担。

And they often have access to parents' social networks to help them find jobs.

而且他们往往还能借助父母的社交网络来求职。

Surveys revealed that working-class and first-generation college graduates, regardless of their career field, are more likely than their wealthier peers to end up in low-paying unskilled jobs when they pursue their passion.

调查显示，工薪家庭出身和第一代大学毕业生，无论从事何种职业领域，在追随激情时，都比家境优越的同龄人更有可能最终落入低薪、无技能的工作岗位。

P9

Colleges, workplaces and career counselors who promote the "follow your passion" path for everyone, without leveling the playing field, help perpetuate socioeconomic inequalities among career aspirants.

高校、职场和职业顾问若在不营造公平竞争环境的情况下，向所有人鼓吹“追随你的激情”这条路，就会助长求职者之间社会经济不平等的延续。

P10

It's not just well-off passion-seekers who benefit from the passion principle.

从激情原则中获益的并不只是家境优渥的追梦者。

Employers of passionate workers do, too.

雇用富有激情员工的雇主同样从中受益。

Potential employers showed greater interest in passionate applicants in part because they believed the applicants would work hard at their jobs without expecting an increase in pay.

潜在雇主对满怀激情的求职者表现出更浓厚的兴趣，部分原因在于他们相信这些求职者会努力工作，而不会要求加薪。

They even sacrifice a good salary, job stability and leisure time to work in a job they love.

他们甚至愿意牺牲优厚的薪酬、稳定的工作和闲暇时间，去从事自己热爱的工作。

第 2 套 · 4 篇 · 共 2,376 词

Section A · 选词填空（填空已还原）288 词 · 16 句

词库（绿 = 本题所选）：A) adaptationsB) blastedC) catastrophicD) evolvedE) featF) interiorG) probingH) recurrenceI) rigorsJ) seeminglyK) surviveL) temptM) thrillN) unanimouslyO) unfolding

No beast on Earth is tougher than the tiny tardigrade.

地球上没有哪种动物比小小的水熊虫更顽强。

It can survive being frozen at -272° Celsius, being exposed to the vacuum of outer space and even being blasted with 500 times the dose of X-rays that would kill a human.

它能在-272摄氏度的极寒中存活，能暴露于外太空的真空环境，甚至能承受相当于致死剂量500倍的X射线轰击。

In other words, the creature can endure conditions that don't even exist on Earth.

换言之，这种生物能忍受地球上根本不存在的极端环境。

And researchers are looking to the microscopic animals to learn how to prepare humans and crops to handle the rigors of space travel.

科学家们正寄望于这种微小动物，探索如何帮助人类和农作物做好准备，以应对太空旅行的严酷考验。

The tardigrade's indestructibility stems from its adaptations to its environment—which may seem surprising, since it lives in seemingly comfortable places, like the cool, wet patches of moss that dot a garden wall.

水熊虫的坚不可摧源于它对环境的适应——这或许令人意外，因为它栖息在看似舒适的环境中，比如点缀于花园墙面上的一片片阴凉潮湿的苔藓。

But it turns out that a tardigrade's damp, mossy home can dry out many times each year.

但事实证明，水熊虫那潮湿的苔藓之家每年会多次经历干枯。

Drying is pretty catastrophic for most living things.

脱水对大多数生物而言堪称灾难。

It does damage to cells in some of the same ways that freezing, vacuum and radiation do.

它对细胞造成的损害在某些方面与冷冻、真空和辐射如出一辙。

Tardigrades, however, have evolved special strategies for dealing with these kinds of damage.

然而，水熊虫已经进化出应对这类损伤的特殊策略。

As a tardigrade dries out, its cells produce several strange proteins that are unlike anything found in other animals.

当水熊虫脱水时，其细胞会产生几种奇特的蛋白质，这些蛋白质在其他动物体内绝无仅有。

In water, the proteins are shapeless.

在水中，这些蛋白质毫无固定形态。

But as water disappears, the proteins self-assemble into long fibers that fill the cell's interior.

但随着水分消失，这些蛋白质会自行组装成长纤维，填满细胞内部。

The fibers support the cell's membranes and proteins, preventing them from breaking or unfolding.

这些纤维支撑着细胞的膜和蛋白质，防止它们破裂或解折叠。

Emulating tardigrades could one day help humans colonize outer space.

模仿水熊虫的特性，有朝一日或能帮助人类开拓外太空。

Food crops could be engineered to produce tardigrade proteins, allowing these organisms to grow more efficiently on spacecraft where levels of radiation are elevated compared with on Earth.

可以通过基因工程改造粮食作物，使其产生水熊虫蛋白，从而让这些作物在太空飞行器上更高效地生长，因为太空飞行器上的辐射水平高于地球上。

So if humans ever succeed in reaching the stars, they may accomplish this feat, in part, by standing on the shoulders of the tiny eight-legged endurance specialists in your backyard.

因此，如果人类有朝一日成功抵达群星，那么这一壮举在一定程度上是站在了你家后院里那些微小的八条腿耐力专家的肩膀上。

Section B · 长篇阅读（信息匹配）1194 词 · 60 句

A

As the scale and impacts of climate change become increasingly alarming, meat is a popular target for action.

随着气候变化的规模和影响日益令人担忧，肉类成为减排行动的热门靶点。

Advocates for the protection of the natural environment from destruction or pollution urge the public to eat less meat.

倡导保护自然环境免遭破坏或污染的人士呼吁公众少吃肉。

Some activists have even called for taxing meat to reduce consumption of it.

一些活动人士甚至呼吁对肉类征税以减少消费。

B

A key claim underlying these arguments holds that globally, meat production generates more greenhouse gases than the entire transportation sector.

这些论点背后的一个核心主张是，从全球范围来看，肉类生产所产生的温室气体超过了整个交通运输行业。

However, this claim is demonstrably wrong, as I will show.

然而，正如我将要证明的那样，这一说法显然是错误的。

And its persistence has led to false assumptions about the linkage between meat and climate change.

而这一说法的持续流传，导致人们对肉类与气候变化之间的关联产生了错误的假设。

C

My recent research focuses on ways in which animal agriculture affects air quality and climate change.

我近期的研究聚焦于畜牧业影响空气质量与气候变化的方式。

In my view, there are many reasons for either choosing animal protein or opting for a vegetarian selection.

在我看来，选择动物蛋白或选择素食，都有许多理由。

However, abandoning meat and meat products is not the environmental panacea many would have us believe.

然而，放弃肉类及肉制品并非许多人试图让我们相信的万能环保良方。

And if taken to an extreme, it also could have harmful nutritional consequences.

而且，如果走向极端，还可能带来有害的营养后果。

D

A healthy portion of meat's negative reputation centers on the assertion that livestock is the largest source of greenhouse gases worldwide.

肉类的负面名声有很大一部分源于这样一种断言：牲畜是全球最大的温室气体来源。

For example, an analysis published in 2009 by the World Watch Institute based in Washington, D.C.

例如，2009年总部位于华盛顿特区的世界观察研究所发布的一项分析。

asserted that 51 percent of global greenhouse gas emissions come from rearing and processing livestock.

断言称，全球51%的温室气体排放来自牲畜的饲养与加工。

According to the U.S. Environmental Protection Agency, the largest sources of U.S. greenhouse gas emissions in 2016 were electricity production (28 percent of total emissions), transportation (28 percent) and industry (22 percent).

据美国环境保护署（EPA）统计，2016年美国温室气体排放的最大来源是电力生产（占排放总量的28%）、交通运输（28%）和工业（22%）。

All of agriculture accounted for a total of 9 percent, but all of animal agriculture contributes less than half of this amount, representing 3.9 percent of the total greenhouse emission in the U.S. That is very different from claiming that livestock represents as much as or more than transportation.

整个农业部门合计占9%，而其中整个畜牧业的贡献还不到这一数字的一半，仅占美国温室气体排放总量的3.9%。这与所谓“畜牧业的排放量与交通运输业相当甚至更多”的说法相去甚远。

E

Why is there such a misconception?

为什么会出现这样的误解？

In 2006 the United Nations Food and Agriculture Organization (FAO) published a study titled "Livestock's Long Shadow," which received widespread international attention.

2006年，联合国粮食及农业组织（粮农组织，FAO）发布了一份题为《牲畜的巨大阴影》的研究报告，在国际上引起了广泛关注。

It stated that livestock produced a staggering 18 percent of the world's greenhouse gas emissions.

报告称，畜牧业产生了高达全球18%的温室气体排放。

The agency drew a startling conclusion that livestock was doing more to harm the climate than all modes of transportation combined.

该机构得出了一个惊人的结论：畜牧业对气候的破坏超过了所有交通运输方式的总和。

This latter claim was wrong, and has since been corrected by Henning Stenfeld, the report's senior author.

后一种说法是错误的，报告的第一作者亨宁·斯滕菲尔德后来已予以更正。

F

The problem was that analysts from the FAO used a comprehensive life-cycle assessment to study the climate impact of livestock, but a different method when they analyzed transportation.

问题在于，粮农组织的分析人员在研究畜牧业的气候影响时采用了全面的生命周期评估方法，而在分析交通运输时却使用了另一套方法。

For livestock, they considered every factor associated with producing meat.

对于畜牧业，他们考虑了与肉类生产相关的每一个环节。

This included emissions from fertilizer production, converting land from forests to pastures, growing feed, and direct emissions from animals (manure as well as expelling of gas from the stomach) from birth to death.

这包括化肥生产、将森林改为牧场、种植饲料所产生的排放，以及牲畜从出生到死亡的直接排放（包括粪便和胃部排气）。

G

However, when they looked at transportation's carbon footprint, they ignored impacts on the climate from manufacturing vehicle materials and parts, assembling vehicles and maintaining roads, bridges and airports.

然而，在计算交通运输的碳足迹时，他们却忽略了制造车辆材料与零部件、组装车辆以及维护道路、桥梁和机场所产生的气候影响。

Instead, they only considered the exhaust smoke emitted by finished cars, trucks, trains and planes.

相反，他们只考虑了成品轿车、卡车、火车和飞机所排放的尾气。

As a result, the FAO's comparison of greenhouse gas emissions from livestock to those from transportation was greatly distorted.

结果，粮农组织对畜牧业温室气体排放量与交通运输温室气体排放量的比较被严重扭曲。

H

I pointed out this flaw during a speech to fellow scientists in San Francisco on March 22, 2010, which led to a flood of media coverage.

2010年3月22日，我在旧金山向同行科学家发表演讲时指出了这一缺陷，此事随后引发了大量媒体报道。

To its credit, the FAO immediately owned up to its error.

值得肯定的是，粮农组织立即承认了自己的错误。

Unfortunately, the agency's initial claim that livestock was responsible for the lion's share of world greenhouse gas emissions had already received wide coverage.

遗憾的是，该机构最初声称畜牧业是全球温室气体排放的主要来源，这一说法已经广为流传。

To this day, we struggle to "unring" the bell.

时至今日，我们仍在努力消除这一错误说法的不良影响。

In its most recent assessment report, the FAO estimated that livestock produces 14.5 percent of global greenhouse gas emissions from human activities.

在其最新评估报告中，粮农组织估计，畜牧业产生的温室气体占全球人类活动温室气体排放量的14.5%。

There is no comparable full life-cycle assessment for transportation.

交通运输方面则没有类似的全生命周期评估。

However, as Stenfeld has pointed out, direct emissions from transportation versus livestock can be compared and amount to 14 versus 5 percent, respectively.

然而，正如斯坦菲尔德所指出的，交通运输与畜牧业的直接排放量是可以比较的，分别占14%和5%。

I

Many people continue to think that avoiding meat as infrequently as once a week will make a significant difference to the climate.

许多人仍然认为，哪怕只是每周少吃一次肉，也会对气候产生显著影响。

But according to one recent study, even if Americans eliminated all animal protein from their diets, they would reduce U.S. greenhouse gas emissions by only 2.6 percent.

但根据最近的一项研究，即使美国人完全从饮食中剔除所有动物蛋白，也只能使美国的温室气体排放量减少2.6%。

According to our research at the University of California, Davis, if the practice of Meatless Monday were to be adopted by all Americans, we'd see a reduction of only 0.5 percent.

根据我们在加州大学戴维斯分校的研究，如果全美推广"无肉星期一"的做法，排放量也只会减少0.5%。

J

Moreover, technological, genetic and management changes that have taken place in U.S. agriculture over the past 70 years have made livestock production more efficient and less greenhouse gas-intensive.

此外，过去70年间美国农业在技术、基因和管理方面发生的变化，使畜牧业生产效率更高，温室气体排放强度更低。

According to the FAO's statistical database, total direct greenhouse gas emissions from U.S. livestock have declined by 11.3 percent since 1961, while production of livestock meat has more than doubled.

据联合国粮农组织统计数据库显示，自1961年以来，美国畜牧业的温室气体直接排放总量下降了11.3%，而畜禽肉产量却增长了一倍以上。

K

Demand for meat is rising in developing and emerging economies, especially in the Middle East, North Africa and Southeast Asia.

发展中和新兴经济体对肉类的需求正在上升，尤其是在中东、北非和东南亚地区。

For example, raising livestock such as goats in Kenya is an important source of food and income for many small-scale farmers and herders.

例如，在肯尼亚饲养山羊等牲畜是许多小规模农户和牧民的重要食物和收入来源。

But meat consumption per person in these regions still lags that of developed countries.

但这些地区的人均肉类消费量仍落后于发达国家。

In 2015, average annual meat consumption per person in developed countries was 92 kilograms, compared to 24 kilograms in the Middle East and North Africa and 18 kilograms in Southeast Asia.

2015年，发达国家人均年肉类消费量为92公斤，而中东和北非为24公斤，东南亚为18公斤。

Still, given projected population growth in the developing world, there will certainly be an opportunity for countries such as the United States to bring their sustainable livestock rearing practices to the table.

尽管如此，鉴于发展中世界预计的人口增长，美国等国家无疑有机会将其可持续畜牧养殖实践推向国际舞台。

L

Removing animals from U.S. agriculture would lower national greenhouse gas emissions to a small degree, but it would also make it harder to meet people's nutritional requirements.

将牲畜从美国农业中移除，会在一定程度上降低全国温室气体排放，但也会使满足人们的营养需求变得更加困难。

Many critics of animal agriculture are quick to point out that if farmers raised only plants, they could produce more pounds of food and more calories per person.

许多反对畜牧业的批评者很快指出，如果农民只种植作物，就能产出更多磅数的食物和更多的人均热量。

But humans also need many essential micro- and macro-nutrients for good health.

但人类要保持健康，还需要许多必需的微量和宏量营养素。

It's hard to make a compelling argument that the United States has a calorie deficit, given its high national rates of adult and child obesity.

鉴于美国成人和儿童肥胖率居高不下，很难令人信服地论证美国存在热量不足的问题。

Moreover, not all plant parts are edible or desirable.

此外，并非所有植物部位都可食用或值得食用。

Raising livestock is a way to add nutritional and economic value to plant agriculture.

饲养牲畜是为种植业增添营养价值和经济价值的一种方式。

M

As one example, the energy in plants that livestock consume is most often contained in cellulose, which is indigestible for humans and many other mammals.

举例来说，牲畜所食用的植物中的能量，大多储存在纤维素中，而纤维素是人类和许多其他哺乳动物无法消化的。

But cows, sheep and other ruminant animals can break cellulose down and release the solar energy contained in this vast resource.

然而，牛、羊及其他反刍动物却能分解纤维素，释放出这一庞大资源中所蕴含的太阳能。

According to the FAO, as much as 70 percent of all agricultural land globally is range land that can only be utilized as grazing land for ruminant livestock.

据联合国粮农组织（FAO）统计，全球高达70%的农业用地属于草原，只能用作反刍牲畜的放牧地。

N

The world population is currently projected to reach 9.8 billion by 2050.

据预测，到2050年世界人口将达到98亿。

Feeding this many people will raise immense challenges.

养活如此众多的人口将带来巨大挑战。

Meat is more calorie-dense per serving than vegetarian options, and ruminant animals largely thrive on feed that is not suitable for humans.

每份肉类的热量密度高于素食选择，而且反刍动物主要靠不适宜人类食用的饲料茁壮成长。

Raising livestock also offers much-needed income for small-scale farmers in developing nations.

饲养牲畜还能为发展中国家的小农户提供急需的收入。

Worldwide, livestock provides a livelihood for 1 billion people.

在全球范围内，牲畜养殖为10亿人提供了生计。

O

Climate change demands urgent attention, and the livestock industry has a large overall environmental footprint that affects air, water and land.

气候变化亟待关注，而畜牧业的整体环境足迹巨大，影响着空气、水和土地。

These, combined with a rapidly rising world population, give us plenty of compelling reasons to continue to work for greater efficiencies in animal agriculture.

这些问题，再加上世界人口的迅速增长，为我们继续努力提高畜牧业效率提供了诸多令人信服的理由。

I believe the place to start is with science-based facts.

我认为，起点应当是以科学为依据的事实。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 1448 词 · 20 句

P1

Why are we so worried about our careers?

我们为何如此担忧自己的事业？

Partly it's to do with money, but there's a psychological aspect to our fears as well.

部分原因与金钱有关，但我们的恐惧也有心理层面的因素。

P2

We worry because we suspect—not wrongly—that the world is full of a frightening sort of person ready to judge us ruthlessly and swiftly: a person we can call a snob.

我们之所以忧心，是因为我们怀疑——而且并非怀疑错了——世上满是这样一种可怕的人：他们随时准备毫不留情、迅速果断地评判我们；这种人可以称作势利者。

P3

A snob is anyone who takes a relatively small part of us and uses it to come to a rigid conclusion about how much of their attention we deserve.

势利者就是那种抓住我们身上相对微小的一部分，便据此对我们应得他们多少关注下定论的人。

In the past, that might be your ancestry and royal connections.

在过去，这一点或许是你的出身和王室关系。

Nowadays, the snob cares about one thing only: what you do for a living.

而如今，势利者只在乎一件事：你是靠什么谋生的。

P4

This explains why the first question we will be asked in any new social context is 'What do you do?

这就解释了，为何在任何新的社交场合，人们问我们的第一个问题总是"你是做什么的？"

' and according to how we answer, snobs will either welcome us with broad smiles, or leave us in the cold.

而根据我们的回答，势利者要么满面笑容地欢迎我们，要么对我们冷眼相待。

P5

And that is why we are fired up by such a desperate urge to achieve and impress.

正因如此，我们才会被一种不顾一切想要成功、想要给人留下深刻印象的强烈冲动所驱使。

P6

Sometimes our behaviour is mistaken for greed and vanity, but it is more than this.

有时我们的行为被误认为是贪婪和虚荣，但其含义不止于此。

A lot of our interest in fancy cars, jobs and houses has nothing to do with materialism.

我们对豪车、名职、豪宅的诸多向往，与物质主义毫无关系。

It has to do with a hunger for the respect and esteem that is only available in our societies through the acquisition of material goods.

它源于一种对尊重和敬意的渴求，而在我们的社会里，唯有获取物质财富才能换来这种尊重和敬意。

It isn't the goods themselves we seek, it is the love we stand to gain through our possession of them.

我们所追求的并非商品本身，而是通过拥有它们所能获得的爱。

The next time we see someone driving a Ferrari, we shouldn't condemn them for their greed, we should pity them for the intensity of their need for love from the world.

下次当我们看到有人开着法拉利时，不该指责他们的贪婪，而应怜悯他们对来自世界的爱有着如此强烈的渴求。

P7

At the root of snobbery is a lack of imagination and confidence about how to decide who in the world is valuable.

势利的根源，在于缺乏想象力和自信，不知道该如何判断世上什么人才是真正有价值的。

The snobs are brutally misguided and slavish in their beliefs about how the superior individuals can be identified.

势利者在如何辨识出类拔萃之人这一问题上，既被严重误导，又盲从迷信。

For snobs, it is the already acclaimed and already successful who are the only ones worthy of respect.

在势利者眼中，唯有那些早已声名显赫、早已功成名就之人才配得上尊重。

There is no room in their timid regimented minds to imagine that someone might be clever, kind or good—and yet somehow have been overlooked entirely by society, their qualities lying hidden beneath an unfamiliar veil, and having as yet discovered no obvious outlet.

他们怯懦而刻板的头脑里，容不下这样的想象：一个人或许聪明、善良、品行端正——却不知何故被社会完全忽视，其品质隐没在一层陌生的面纱之下，至今尚未找到显而易见的施展之处。

P8

The true answer to snobbery is not to say that there is no such thing as a better or worse person, but to insist that better or worse exist in constantly unexpected places and carry none of the outward signs of distinction.

应对势利的真正之道，并非宣称人无所谓优劣之分，而是坚持认为：优劣存在于种种出人意料的地方，且不带任何外在的显赫标志。

And because we are such poor judges of the worth of others, our ultimate duty remains to be kind, good, curious and imaginative about pretty much everyone who ever crosses our path.

正因为我们如此不善于评判他人的价值，我们的终极责任便是对几乎每一个与我们不期而遇的人保持善意、良善、好奇与想象力。

Section C · 仔细阅读 Passage 2446 词 · 20 句

P1

Women have historically been paid less.

在历史上，女性的薪酬一直低于男性。

But in the US in the 1980s, they began to catch up—fast.

但在20世纪80年代的美国，女性的收入开始迅速追赶上来。

During that decade, the gender pay gap closed by about one percentage point a year.

在那十年间，性别薪酬差距每年缩小约一个百分点。

Had that trend continued, the gender wage gap would have been closed by 2017.

如果这一趋势延续下去，到2017年性别薪酬差距本可基本消除。

P2

But the trend didn't continue, and the gap remains yawning.

然而这一趋势并未延续，薪酬差距依然悬殊。

P3

According to a new study from academics at Harvard, the stagnation can be put down, perhaps counterintuitively, to the introduction of state and federal family leave policies.

哈佛大学学者的一项新研究认为，这种停滞或许有悖直觉，却可归因于各州及联邦家庭休假政策的出台。

P4

The academics argue that during the 1990s, as governments began to introduce leave policies, it was mainly women who took advantage of them.

这些学者指出，20世纪90年代各国政府开始推行休假政策时，主要利用这些政策的是女性。

Though the leave policies might have helped those women to stay in the workplace—instead of dropping out to have families—those who returned saw their wages had increased at lower rates than the men.

尽管休假政策可能帮助这些女性留在职场——而不是离职回家生儿育女——但重返岗位后，她们的工资涨幅却低于男性。

P5

After family leave was introduced in the US, in fact, the rate of gender wage convergence fell to just 0.03 percentage points per year, and has remained there ever since.

事实上，美国引入家庭休假制度后，性别薪酬趋同的速度降至每年仅0.03个百分点，并从此一直维持在这一水平。

P6

Those monitoring the process towards salary equity at work have long watched as progress slowed in many countries around the world.

那些关注职场薪酬平等进程的人早就注意到，全球许多国家的进展已趋于放缓。

In fact, that progress began to reverse during the pandemic.

事实上，这一进展在疫情期间开始逆转。

P7

The gender pay gap is one of the most outstanding examples of that lack of parity, and still exists just about everywhere.

性别薪酬差距是这种不平等最突出的表现之一，几乎在所有地方都依然存在。

The motherhood penalty has become a shorthand for describing why: In many places, especially rich countries, women earn the same as men until they reach their childbearing years.

母职惩罚已成为描述其原因的简明说法：在许多地方，尤其是富裕国家，女性在到达生育年龄之前，收入与男性持平。

Women who have children begin to see their salaries slip behind their male counterparts.

生育了孩子的女性开始发现，自己的薪水逐渐落后于男性同行。

P8

Part of this is because women take on more of the unpaid labor at home, which can eat into time available for work and energy for career advancement.

部分原因在于女性承担了更多家庭中的无酬劳动，这会挤占可用于工作的时间和职业发展所需的精力。

But it's also because mothers are passed over for raises and promotion, and because time out of the workplace sets women back, even if that time is taken voluntarily, and supported by company or government policy.

但另一部分原因是，母亲们在加薪和晋升时被跳过，而且脱离职场的时间会使女性落后，哪怕这段时间是自愿休假，并且得到了公司或政府政策的支持。

P9

What would have happened if leave policies hadn't been introduced?

如果当时没有出台休假政策，情况会怎样？

The study doesn't go into that question, other than to say that if the 1980s trend continued, we would have been at parity by now.

该研究并未深入探讨这一问题，只是指出，如果20世纪80年代的趋势得以延续，我们现在已经实现了薪酬平等。

P10

It's possible, however, that the journey towards wage parity would have stalled either way.

然而，无论采取哪种方式，通往薪酬平等的道路都可能陷入停滞。

If women's gains in the 1980s were made through the erasure of things like bias, once those less uncontrollable problems had been addressed, there would still have been an issue with women—who are the ones to bear children and take care of them in the early weeks because of biological factors like the ability to breastfeed, forcing them to take breaks, whether or not those breaks were mandated.

如果女性在20世纪80年代的收入增长是通过消除偏见等问题实现的，那么一旦那些较不可控的问题得到解决，依然会面临一个核心难题——女性由于母乳喂养等生理因素，是生育孩子并在最初几周照料孩子的人，这迫使她们必须中断工作，无论这种中断是否由制度规定。

第 3 套 · 4 篇 · 共 2,368 词

Section A · 选词填空（填空已还原）282 词 · 14 句

词库（绿 = 本题所选）：A) bolstersB) consequencesC) credentialsD) dramaticallyE) enactF) equityG) especiallyH) evasivelyI) formidableJ) impetusK) lavishL) prioritizeM) sufficesN) sustainedO) tenured

The slow progress of women in elective office is frustrating for some political observers and experts—including myself.

女性在民选公职中的缓慢进展，令一些政治观察家和专家感到沮丧——我本人也不例外。
